However, most architectures maintain a constant width across all layers, allocating a fixed parameter and computation budget evenly despite different layers potentially playing distinct computational roles. In this work, we empirically investigate nonuniform capacity allocation across network depth by proposing a $\\times$-shaped > <former architecture. This design maintains wid","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:59:03Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18246.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文问的是一个很具体但长期被默认处理的问题：在固定深度和参数预算下，Transformer 的每一层是否都应该使用相同 hidden width。现有 scaling 研究通常比较深度、宽度、总参数量的全局比例，但很少改变层内宽度随深度的分布。作者发现，均匀宽度不是一个自然最优点；早期层和晚期层保留更大宽度，中间层收窄的 ×-shaped 配置，在相同参数量下更好。","importance":"对预训练研究的影响在于，它把“模型形状”从全局 depth/width ratio 推进到 layer-wise capacity allocation。这个问题直接关系到 dense LM 和 MoE LM 的参数、FLOPs、KV cache 如何分配。论文给出的收益不只是困惑度改善：在 200M–2B dense 和 3B MoE 设置下，×-shaped 结构相对同参数 uniform baseline 约有 3% 相对 perplexity 改善，同时 KV cache 约少 10%，FLOPs 约少 3%。这个量级不算大，但方向清楚，而且与 serving 成本相关。更重要的是，它提示预训练架构搜索不应只搜索总宽度和深度，还应搜索宽度沿深度的 profile。","technical_approach":"作者提出 Variable-Width Transformer，并重点验证一种早晚宽、中间窄的 ×-shaped 结构，称为 > <former。对比的形状包括 growing 的 ∨-shaped、narrowing 的 ∧-shaped、先宽后窄或先窄后宽的其它 profile。关键实现不是简单在层间加 projection，而是维护一个固定的全局 residual stream，宽度等于最宽层。每个 block 只读写 residual stream 的一个 slice；当前层不用的坐标绕过该层，后续需要扩展时从最近一次处理过该坐标的层恢复。收窄时截断到当前层宽度，扩宽时复制此前保存的坐标；如果此前不存在该坐标，则补零。这个设计避免了可学习 projection 带来的瓶颈和额外参数，也尽量保持 residual path 的语义。与只调整 FFN intermediate dimension 的做法不同，这里调整的是整个 block 的模型宽度，因此 attention、MLP、KV cache 和激活搬运都会一起改变。论文还分析了为什么同参数匹配时平均层宽会下降：参数量近似随宽度平方增长，而 attention FLOPs 和 KV cache 更接近随宽度线性增长，所以不均匀宽度可以在保留总参数量的同时降低平均 KV 宽度。","experiments":"实验覆盖 decoder-only LM，dense 规模从 200M 到 2B 参数，另有 3B 参数 MoE。主结果是 > <former 在参数匹配的 uniform-width baseline 上稳定降低语言建模 perplexity，平均约 3% 相对改善，同时约减少 10% KV-cache size 和约 3% FLOPs。作者还做了多种 shape 对比，结论是早晚层更宽、中间层更窄优于单调增宽、单调收窄等 profile。残差 resize 的消融很关键：固定全局 residual stream 加 slice/copy 的参数无关方案优于可学习 projection 和简单 zero padding，说明收益不是来自额外映射层，而是来自保留绕过中间窄层的残差信息。论文还讨论了 bottleneck width 和 bottleneck location 与模型预算的关系，并给出经验指导：瓶颈不能过窄，位置也不能任意移动；合适的中层收窄能减少 mid-layer representation collapse。表示分析显示，> <former 学到的表示策略不同于 uniform baseline，中间层的表示坍缩程度更低。","reading_guide":"建议精读三部分。第一是 Variable-Width Transformer 的 residual resizing 设计，这是能否复现结果的核心；重点看固定 residual stream、slice 读写、截断和复制恢复的定义，以及为什么不用 projection。第二是主实验和 shape 对比，重点确认参数匹配方式、FLOPs/KV cache 计算方式，以及 ×-shaped 相对其它 profile 的稳定性。第三是消融和表示分析，尤其是 resize 策略、bottleneck width/location、mid-layer representation collapse。对做大模型预训练的人，最值得深挖的是两点：一是宽度 profile 是否能与 depth scaling law 联合建模；二是这种结构在长上下文、GQA/MQA、MoE routing、训练稳定性上是否仍保持同样收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a simple architectural assumption that is rarely isolated: under a fixed depth and parameter budget, should every Transformer layer have the same hidden width. Most scaling work varies global depth, width, and parameter count, while keeping width constant across layers. The paper finds that this uniform allocation is not necessarily optimal. A profile with wider early and late layers and narrower middle layers performs better at matched parameter count.","importance":"The main contribution is layer-wise capacity allocation for pretraining-scale decoder LMs. It extends the notion of model shape beyond a global depth-width ratio and makes it a function over depth. The result is relevant to both training and inference: across 200M–2B dense LMs and a 3B MoE LM, the proposed ×-shaped design improves perplexity by about 3% relative to parameter-matched uniform baselines, while reducing KV-cache size by about 10% and FLOPs by about 3%. The gains are modest but operationally meaningful, because they affect both pretraining loss and serving cost. The broader lesson is that architecture scaling laws should not assume uniform width by default.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"The experiments train decoder-only language models from 200M to 2B dense parameters, plus a 3B-parameter MoE setting. The ×-shaped > <former consistently outperforms parameter-matched constant-width baselines, with roughly 3% relative perplexity improvement. Because parameters scale roughly quadratically with width while attention FLOPs and KV-cache size scale more linearly, parameter matching leads to a lower average layer width; the reported outcome is about 10% smaller KV cache and about 3% fewer FLOPs. Shape comparisons show that the early-and-late-wide profile is better than monotonic growing, monotonic narrowing, and other nonuniform alternatives. Ablations on resizing are important: the fixed-residual slice/copy mechanism beats learned projection and naive zero-padding variants. Additional analyses study bottleneck width and location, and suggest that the architecture uses a different representation strategy while reducing mid-layer representation collapse.","reading_guide":"Read the residual resizing mechanism carefully first; it is the key engineering idea behind the result. Then inspect the main shape-comparison experiments and check how parameter matching, FLOPs, and KV-cache size are computed. The ablations on learned projections, zero padding, bottleneck width, and bottleneck location are the most useful parts for replication. The representation-collapse analysis is also worth reading, because it gives a possible explanation for why narrowing the middle layers does not simply remove useful capacity. compu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:37:27Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18208.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是世界模型里一个很具体但长期存在的矛盾：长时程预测通常需要更深的计算，才能在每一步状态转移上做足够细的推断；但把模型直接做深，会同时抬高参数量、部署成本和训练难度，而且 rollout 越长，误差越容易累积。作者把这个问题改写成“单步转移是否可以通过共享参数的迭代细化来完成”，也就是不靠堆更多独立层，而是对同一个 latent transition operator 反复应用，让模型在固定参数预算下增加‘内循环深度’。论文的关键点不只是把 looped transformer 搬到 world model，而是把环境动力学中的重复作用规律，与共享更新算子的迭代 refinement 对齐，并进一步处理了循环深度增加后数值不稳定的问题。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接相关性不在纯文本 LLM，而在“深度是否必须等于参数量”这个更一般的建模问题。它提出了 iterative latent depth 作为新的 scaling 轴：模型容量不只靠参数、数据和上下文长度扩展，还可以靠测试时增加循环次数来提升单步推断质量。这一点和 looped LM、test-time compute、latent reasoning 的研究方向是连着的。若实验结论成立，意义主要有三层。第一，参数共享的深度递归在时序生成模型里也能成立，不局限于语言。第二，world model 这种对 rollout stability 很敏感的场景，反而更能检验 looped architecture 是否真的学到“迭代求精”而不是浅层拟合。第三，它给统一生成模型一个启发：对于视频、交互环境、甚至多模态 token 序列，内循环计算可能是比简单加深 backbone 更经济的扩展方式。量级上，这篇更像范式验证和 scaling 方向探索，不是已经定型的通用 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Looped World Model：用一个参数共享的 transformer block，对同一时刻的 latent state 进行多次迭代更新，而不是用一串彼此独立的深层 block 做一次前向。这样单步状态转移被拆成“外层时间推进 + 内层迭代 refinement”两层结构。外层仍然是 world model 的时间演化；内层则对下一状态的隐表示反复修正。\n\n从文中描述看，作者借鉴了近期 looped transformer 的稳定化做法，在循环更新里引入了带谱约束的 state-retention parameterization。直观上，它把一部分更新写成收缩性的线性保留项，再叠加输入注入和 transformer 非线性项。这样做不是为了表达某个精确物理方程，而是为了保证当内循环次数增加时，latent trajectory 不会因为共享块反复作用而发散。这个设计和普通 residual stack 的区别在于：普通深层 transformer 的每一层参数不同，稳定性主要靠初始化、归一化和优化过程；这里因为同一个块被反复调用，状态转移矩阵的谱性质会直接决定深度扩展时是否稳定，所以需要显式约束。\n\n另一个重要点是 adaptive computation。论文强调不同预测步的难度不同，因此不必给每个时间步分配相同的内循环深度。LoopWM 可以按样本或按时间步自适应决定迭代次数，让复杂转移多算几步，简单转移少算几步。这和固定深度 world model 的差别很大：后者把所有状态转移都用同样计算预算处理，容易在简单步上浪费算力、在困难步上算力不足。\n\n从建模立场看，这篇不是 diffusion world model，也不是单纯 RSSM/AR transformer 的替代，而是给现有 backbone 增加一个“共享参数的深度维度”。因此它与扩大模型宽度、增加训练数据、延长上下文是正交的。作者也明确把 iterative latent depth 视为新的 scaling axis。","experiments":"从引言能确认的核心结果有两点。第一，参数效率提升最高可到 100×，这是论文最醒目的数字。这里应理解为：在达到相近世界建模效果时，looped 架构所需独立参数量远低于常规深层方案，而不是同等 FLOPs 下无条件提升 100×。第二，模型支持 adaptive computation，推理时可按预测复杂度动态扩展内循环深度。\n\n不过，当前给出的全文片段没有完整实验表和具体 benchmark 数字，因此无法可靠复述更细的结果，例如使用了哪些环境、latent tokenizer 形式、与 Dreamer/IRIS/EMERALD 等基线相比的绝对分数、不同 loop 次数下的 rollout error 曲线、以及谱约束对稳定性的消融幅度。若按论文叙事推断，最值得确认的实验应包括：1）固定参数量下与非共享深层 transformer/RSSM 的比较；2）固定训练预算下，增加 loop 次数是否持续改善长时程 rollout；3）训练时 loop 深度与测试时 loop 深度不一致时，是否仍有 test-time compute scaling；4）没有谱约束时是否出现发散、性能回退或梯度不稳；5）adaptive halting 相比固定 loop 的收益是否主要来自效率还是也改善质量。\n\n如果精读全文，建议重点提取三类数字：参数量与性能的 Pareto 曲线、长时程 rollout 指标随 loop 深度变化的曲线、以及不同环境复杂度下的自适应计算分配统计。这三类结果决定这篇工作到底是“省参数”，还是“真的引入了新的可扩展计算轴”。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精读之间的方式。先看引言里作者如何定义问题：不是单纯做更小模型，而是把世界模型的单步预测改成迭代 refinement。然后直接看方法部分关于 looped update、state-retention parameterization、谱约束和 adaptive computation 的公式与结构图。这里是整篇最有信息量的地方。\n\n实验部分建议优先看四类图表：1）参数量-性能曲线；2）loop 次数增加时的性能变化；3）长时程 rollout 误差或视频质量退化曲线；4）稳定性消融，尤其是有无谱约束的对比。如果论文提供不同训练 loop 与测试 loop transformer 或 any-order sequence model。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural tension in world modeling: faithful long-horizon simulation often needs deeper computation per transition, but simply making the model deeper increases parameter count, deployment cost, and instability, while rollout errors still compound over time. The proposed reframing is to treat a single transition not as a one-shot forward pass through many distinct layers, but as an iterative refinement process driven by a shared latent update operator. The contribution is not merely “using looped transformers for world models.” The more important claim is that environment dynamics are naturally compatible with repeated application of a shared transition law, and that this can be exploited as an inner computational loop, provided the recurrent latent dynamics are stabilized.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model pretraining is indirect but real. The paper is about a broader scaling question: does more effective depth have to come from more parameters? LoopWM argues for iterative latent depth as an additional scaling axis, alongside model size, data, and context length. That connects naturally to looped language models, test-time compute scaling, and latent reasoning. If the empirical story holds, the contribution matters in three ways. First, parameter-shared depth recursion may be viable beyond language. Second, world models are a stringent testbed because rollout stability is unforgiving; if looping works there, it is stronger evidence than a mild perplexity gain in text. Third, it suggests a path for video, interactive simulation, and unified multimodal sequence models where extra compute is allocated through recurrent refinement rather than a permanently larger backbone. This is more a paradigm probe than a settled recipe.","technical_approach":"$10","experiments":"$11","reading_guide":"$12","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将参数共享循环Transformer迁移至世界建模，迭代隐深度作为正交scaling轴的结论对test-time compute研究有参考，重点看scaling实验部分。","en":"Applies weight-shared looped Transformers to world modeling; the orthogonal iterative latent depth scaling axis is relevant for test-time compute work; focus on scaling experiment sections."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongyuan Adam Lu","Z. The number of effective layers reached by looping determines the quality of the solution these models find. Like deep architectures, looped architectures are prone to a signal propagation problem induced by depth as the halting decision is postponed. In this paper, we address this signal propagation issue using pre-norm layers and residual scaling. Building on th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:36:33Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18206.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理 looped Transformer 的两个核心问题：深度循环后信号传播变差，以及如何在不依赖额外 halting head 或手写 CoT 的情况下，让模型按输入难度自适应分配计算量。","importance":"对预训练和推理架构的启发在于，它把 test-time compute 从序列维度的 CoT 转到深度维度的 latent iteration。FPRM 不是靠生成更长推理链来增加计算，而是在隐藏状态上反复迭代，并用固定点收敛作为停止信号。这类方法对 latent reasoning、循环式 Transformer、以及端到端可训练的自适应计算模型有参考价值。贡献量级更偏架构与训练稳定性，不是大规模语言模型 recipe。","technical_approach":"核心做法有三点。第一，把 looped Transformer 中常见的 post-norm 改成 pre-norm。原因是循环展开后等价于很深的 Transformer，post-norm 虽然能约束激活幅度，但会带来深层信号传播问题；pre-norm 更利于训练深有效深度。第二，引入 residual scaling，同时缩放 residual stream 和 attention/FFN 子层输出，用另一种方式保证循环时激活有界，避免 pre-norm 循环后状态发散。第三，用 fixed-point convergence 做 halting：模型持续迭代，直到相邻隐藏状态变化足够小，收敛本身就是停止信号。与 ACT 类方法不同，它不需要额外学习一个离散停止决策的连续松弛，也不依赖外部控制器。论文还加入了针对固定点模型的稳定化修改，用来限制固定点附近的振荡。","experiments":"实验集中在小参数量 reasoning 模型上，模型规模约 7M。任务包括 Sudoku-Extreme、Maze-Hard、ARC-AGI-1，以及状态跟踪基准 A5 和 S5。论文报告 FPRM 在这些任务上优于同规模 looped reasoning baseline，并且在 Sudoku-Extreme 上以更低计算成本超过 TRM。消融重点支持两个判断：post-norm looped 模型虽然激活更容易有界，但训练深有效层数时信号传播受限；pre-norm 加 residual scaling 能同时改善可训练性和循环稳定性。另一个实验结论是，HRM/TRM 这类带 ACT 或外部 halting 机制的模型没有稳定表现出按样本难度调节计算，而 fixed-point halting 的迭代步数与任务难度更一致。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Method，重点看 pre-norm 与 residual scaling 的设计动机，以及 fixed-point halting 为什么比 ACT 更直接。图 1 可看 FPRM 与 TRM 在 Sudoku-Extreme 上的成本-性能关系；图 3 对理解 bounded activation 很关键；图 4 和图 6 主要看 adaptivity 是否真实发生。附录 B 有 toy looped model 的训练稳定性证据，适合关心深层信号传播的人读。预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses two coupled issues in looped Transformers: signal propagation deteriorates when the loop is unrolled into a very deep effective network, and existing halting mechanisms do not provide clean end-to-end adaptive compute.","importance":"The useful idea for pretraining-oriented research is the shift from sequence-level test-time compute, as in CoT, to depth-level latent computation. FPRM spends more compute by iterating hidden states rather than emitting longer reasoning traces. This is relevant to latent reasoning, recurrent Transformers, and adaptive computation, although the evidence is still on small reasoning models rather than large-scale language model pretraining.","technical_approach":"FPRM makes three main changes. First, it replaces the post-norm layers commonly used in looped models with pre-norm layers. The motivation is that a looped model, once unrolled, behaves like a very deep Transformer, where post-norm is known to hurt signal propagation. Second, it adds residual scaling to keep both the residual stream and sub-layer outputs bounded, so the pre-norm loop does not diverge. Third, it uses convergence to a fixed point as the halting rule: the model keeps iterating until hidden states change little enough. This removes the need for an external ACT-style halting module and avoids training a relaxed discrete stopping decision. The paper also adds a stabilization mechanism to reduce oscillation near the fixed point.","experiments":"The experiments use small reasoning models, around 7M parameters. Benchmarks include Sudoku-Extreme, Maze-Hard, ARC-AGI-1, and state-tracking tasks A5 and S5. The reported results show that FPRM outperforms same-scale looped reasoning baselines and beats TRM on Sudoku-Extreme with lower compute. The ablations support the architectural claim: post-norm keeps activations bounded but limits trainability at large effective depth, while pre-norm plus residual scaling improves signal propagation without losing stability. The paper also shows that ACT-style halting in HRM/TRM does not reliably adapt compute to input difficulty, whereas fixed-point halting produces a clearer difficulty-dependent iteration pattern.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction and Method first. selection is efficient and causal, but the selection step is non-differentiable and fixes the number of active experts in advance. SoftMoE asks whether expert choice and expert count can be optimized directly while keeping token-wise routing and autoregressive compatibility.","importance":"The main relevance for pretraining is in MoE routing and compute allocation. Current sparse MoE recipes usually choose a fixed top-k per layer and then rely on auxiliary balancing losses to keep routing usable. SoftMoE makes the effective k differentiable and adds a global expert budget, allowing the model to redistribute conditional compute across depth. If the behavior holds at larger scale, this changes part of MoE design from static hyperparameter selection to learned budget allocation. The contribution is best viewed as a routing and training-recipe improvement rather than a new language modeling objective.","technical_approach":"SoftMoE replaces the discrete top-k gate with a truncated soft top-k operator based on the LapSum relaxation. Given expert scores from the router, hard top-k produces a binary mask; the proposed relaxation produces continuous expert weights whose total mass corresponds to a possibly non-integer k. The LapSum construction approximates the order-statistics threshold with a smooth function built from Laplace CDFs. This gives a differentiable threshold and avoids sorting or iterative optimization, keeping time and memory linear in the number of experts. A second component parameterizes the mean number of active experts per MoE layer and constrains the global expert budget. Layers can therefore spend more or less expert capacity while the total inference budget remains fixed. Unlike soft-routing methods that mix tokens or let experts select tokens, this design routes each token independently and does not leak future-token information.","experiments":"The reported experiments use language modeling on C4 and OpenWebText, plus downstream evaluations. SoftMoE is reported to match or outperform standard sparse MoE while activating fewer experts on average. The most informative empirical observation is the learned allocation pattern: later layers consistently use more experts, while earlier layers use fewer. This suggests that a uniform top-k across all MoE layers may be an inefficient default. The provided body text does not include the full numerical tables, so exact perplexity, benchmark deltas, model sizes, and throughput numbers cannot be verified here. Important checks are routing entropy, expert-load skew, training-loss stability, and whether inference uses the same soft routing path or an additional hard truncation step.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 20–30 minutes. should use. Looped Transformers reuse the same block to increase latent computation depth without increasing parameter count, but standard sequential looping increases latency and KV-cache memory with the number of loops. PLT reduces this cost with cross-loop position offsets and shared-KV gated sliding-window attention. Once looping becomes cheaper, the key question is whether extra loops still improve representations or instead introduce positional mismatch and redundant computation.","importance":"The paper is relevant to pretraining and architecture research because it treats test-time compute scaling as internal latent computation rather than extra generated reasoning tokens. Its main value is the controlled large-scale comparison: 7B models trained from scratch with matched data, tuning, and evaluation while varying only the loop count. The results show that loop scaling is not monotonic. Two loops help; three loops can hurt badly. This is a useful constraint for recurrent-depth LLM design: parameter sharing and additional computation are not automatically beneficial once the architecture changes the information flow.","technical_approach":"The work builds on the Parallel Loop Transformer. In a conventional looped Transformer, hidden state h^(r) depends on h^(r-1), so loops must be executed sequentially and cache states grow with the loop count. PLT uses cross-loop position offsets to break direct same-token recurrence and enable parallel loop execution, while shared-KV gated sliding-window attention keeps cache growth under control. The paper frames loop-count selection as a gain-cost problem. The gain side measures whether an added loop performs useful refinement through hidden-state movement, attention evolution, and output-distribution shift. The cost side measures positional mismatch introduced by CLP at loop boundaries, summarized by an intrinsic offset cost Ω(r). This makes the analysis more diagnostic than benchmark-only comparisons.","experiments":"LoopCoder-v2 is a family of 7B PLT coding models trained from scratch on 18T mixed text and code tokens, followed by matched instruction tuning. The study compares loop counts R ∈ {1, 2, 3, 4}. The trend is strongly non-monotonic. The two-loop model improves broadly over the non-looped baseline, including SWE-bench Verified increasing from 43.0% to 64.4%. The three-loop model regresses on many tasks and drops to 27.6% on SWE-bench Verified. The internal analysis attributes most productive refinement to the second loop; later loops show diminishing and increasingly oscillatory updates, while CLP-induced offset cost becomes more visible.","reading_guide":"A good skim takes about 30–40 minutes. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T05:03:27Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17524.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注的是推理阶段的内部状态稳定性：LLM 在多步推理里常不是缺知识，而是早期隐藏状态一旦偏了，后续解码会沿着错误方向持续放大。作者试图解决的问题是，能否在不显式生成长链式思维文本的情况下，直接在 latent space 中迭代修正 hidden states，让模型在输出前先把内部表征调整到更稳定、更一致的状态。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接相关性不算强，更接近推理时 latent reasoning / test-time computation 方向。但它有两个启发点。第一，它把“推理增强不一定要靠外显 CoT”这件事做成了一个可训练框架，说明内部状态控制可能是 next-token 解码之外的一条补充路径。第二，它把 RL 用在 hidden-state refinement controller 上，而不是传统 token-level policy，这对研究 pretrain 与 post-train 接口、latent reasoning、隐式思维预算分配的人有参考价值。贡献量级偏方法探索，不像基础训练 recipe 那样会直接改变大规模预训练实践。","technical_approach":"方法名为 ReLAR。整体结构是在一个预训练 Transformer 上外挂一套轻量 latent refinement 模块。输入经过基模型前向后得到最终层 hidden state h0，再通过一个抽取网络压缩成低维 reasoning state s0。之后不立刻解码，而是在 latent space 中执行若干轮 refinement。每一步由两个控制器共同决定：depth controller 决定是否继续 refinement、需要多少步；action controller 决定当前 refinement 的方向或动作。作者把 s_t 设计成一个紧凑瓶颈，两个控制器都只读这个状态，这样所有后续修正都由一个低维摘要来驱动，避免直接在全量 hidden tensor 上做高成本搜索。训练上使用 policy gradient，奖励信号不是最终任务回报，而是 step-wise likelihood improvement，也就是每次 refinement 后目标输出似然是否提升。这个设计的好处是 credit assignment 更细，训练信号更密；同时避免显式 CoT 监督。与已有 latent intervention 工作相比，区别在于它不是一次性编辑 hidden state，也不是静态 steering，而是把 hidden-state revision 做成可迭代、可自适应深度的过程。与 CoT 相比，它不生成中间文本，因此试图用更低的 token 开销换取更稳定的内部推理。","experiments":"从全文片段能确认的实验范围包括 medical reasoning、mathematical reasoning、multi-hop reasoning 和 open-ended generation。作者声称在临床推理 benchmark 上提升了 step-level coherence 和整体可靠性，同时推理时开销低于显式 reasoning baseline。方法层面的关键实验应包括：1）与直接解码基线比较，验证 hidden-state refinement 能减少早期错误传播；2）与 CoT 或其他显式推理方法比较，验证在不生成长推理文本时仍能获得更稳的结果；3）对 depth controller 和 action controller 的消融，检查自适应步数和动作选择是否都必要；4）对 latent state 维度、refinement 步数上限、奖励设计的消融。给出的全文片段没有完整结果表和具体数字，因此无法可靠写出提升幅度、模型规模或数据规模。就论文叙述看，作者更强调 reliability / coherence 与 latency trade-off，而不是绝对任务分数的大幅领先。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题去读：它到底是在做真正的 latent In multi-step problems, an LLM may commit too early to a flawed hidden representation, and subsequent decoding amplifies that error. The central question is whether one can refine hidden states directly, before decoding, so that the model stabilizes its internal representation without relying on explicit chain-of-thought text generation.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to latent reasoning and test-time computation. During mask learning, the binary decisions are relaxed with a hard-concrete parameterization under L0 regularization, and an augmented Lagrangian term enforces the user-specified sparsity target. The method supports layer-wise allocation and finer KV-head-wise allocation. After jointly optimizing mask parameters and model weights, the masks are binarized and fixed for continued pretraining. Compared with hand-crafted interleaving, ConSA optimizes the allocation directly. Compared with calibration-and-ranking methods, it avoids relying on weak scalar scores and enforces the sparsity constraint during training rather than after training.","experiments":"Experiments are reported on 0.6B and 1.7B models across multiple sparsity levels. how much efficiency is lost when prefill workers, decode workers, KV-cache placement, and request routing optimize local objectives, especially near GPU saturation.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to inference-aware model and system design. Its main contribution is an analytical framing for prefill/decode disaggregation, hierarchical KV-cache management, and request routing. Price of Anarchy is used to quantify the gap between greedy local decisions and coordinated global allocation. The work is useful for researchers thinking about long-context serving, KV-cache economics, prefill/decode separation, and latency-cost tradeoffs.","technical_approach":"Using NVIDIA Dynamo as the reference architecture, the paper decomposes disaggregated inference into three coupled games. The first is a two-player resource allocation game between prefill and decode GPU pools, reflecting the conflict between time-to-first-token and inter-token latency. The second is a selfish caching game over hierarchical KV storage, where placing or evicting a block creates future recomputation costs and cache-locality externalities. The third is a congestion game for request routing: sending a request to a cache-warm worker may reduce that request’s cost but can increase congestion for later requests. The paper is explicit that game theory is used for analysis rather than runtime equilibrium computation; practical serving still requires simple low-overhead scheduling and routing mechanisms.","experiments":"The empirical validation focuses on the KV-cache and routing games across three prefill/decode topologies. The prefill/decode resource game is treated analytically rather than validated with the same experimental depth. The central result is a saturation-driven regime change. Below the saturation knee, the routing inefficiency index remains relatively stable. Once the prefill pool becomes overloaded, local greedy routing becomes much less efficient and the Price-of-Anarchy-style gap grows quickly. This supports the claim that disaggregated inference degradation is not merely a smooth utilization effect; the payoff structure changes around saturation.","reading_guide":"Skim rather than read line by line. The most useful parts are the Dynamo abstraction in the Introduction, the three-game formulation in Section 4, and the saturation analysis in Section 5. The experiments are useful for seeing how routing inefficiency and cache behavior vary under different prefill/decode topologies. Section 9.2 is important for understanding the limits of the empirical claims, especially the analytical-only treatment of the P/D resource game. 统计口径。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks why KV cache cannot be locally edited like ordinary memory. When a field inside a long prompt changes, replacing only that field’s key/value vectors does not update the model’s decision; the model still behaves as if the old value were present.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented researchers is the mechanistic view of prefill computation. The model does not merely cache token representations. During prefill, it writes field-conditioned conclusions into later tokens, especially delimiters, newlines, and other aggregator positions. This matters for long-context training, agent memory, prefix caching, compositional prompting, and future models that may expose a more programmable runtime memory.","technical_approach":"The work treats KV cache as a notebook of memoized conclusions. It first builds controlled field-editing setups and compares full recomputation, field-only KV replacement, downstream suffix patching, and note injection. The key finding is causal: the field’s own KV vectors account for less than 1% of the final decision, while downstream cached notes carry nearly all of it. The mechanism is tested with locality patching, suffix concentration, linear probes, and circuit knockouts, plus controls for content-versus-conclusion dissociation, layer timing, specificity, and note injection. On top of this mechanism, the paper proposes two operations. Editing amends stale notes, either with a salient erratum or by recomputing affected downstream notes. Field-only in-place editing is cheap but works reliably mainly when chain-of-thought makes the model reread the field. Composition transplants precompiled skill caches into new contexts by repositioning and splicing cached notes, with seam repair at the boundary.","experiments":"The results are reported across Qwen3, Llama-3.1, Gemma-2, and Mistral families, with additional coverage for MoE models, low-bit quantization, multimodal image caches, MLA, interleaved M-RoPE, and sliding-window attention. The most useful numbers are: field KV contributes under 1% of the decision; with CoT, field-level editing reaches 1.00 decision recovery on an 8B model at roughly 1% of the compute of full recomputation; cache composition gives a 13.9x TTFT improvement at 32k context length, replacing an O(L^2)-like prefill cost with O(L) splicing; in user-memory settings, decision-faithful reuse gives 2.3x to 4.3x lower TTFT and is validated up to 70B and on LoCoMo; in a vLLM online serving benchmark, prefix-cache hit rate increases from about 1% to 98.5%, p90 TTFT drops by 53x to 398x, and throughput improves by up to 14.5x under higher load.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 40 to 60 minutes. post-training quantization for MoE multimodal LLMs. Existing expert-level mixed-precision quantization methods rely on expert activation frequency, but this proxy becomes biased in MoE-MLLMs because vision tokens dominate token counts and many vision tokens are redundant.","importance":"The useful insight is that expert importance in multimodal MoE models cannot be read directly from global routing frequency. Routing statistics are shaped by modality imbalance and by redundancy within the visual stream. This has implications beyond PTQ, including expert pruning, router analysis, load-balancing objectives, and how to interpret expert specialization during multimodal pretraining.","technical_approach":"MODE builds a modality-decomposed expert importance estimate. It separately collects and normalizes expert selection frequencies for text and vision tokens, so text-critical experts are not hidden by the much larger number of visual tokens. On the vision side, it further estimates frequency using only layer-wise key vision tokens, filtering out redundant visual patches that would otherwise cast many near-duplicate router votes. Frequency is not the only signal: MODE also measures quantization sensitivity separately for text tokens and key vision tokens, using output-logit changes such as KL divergence after low-bit quantization. These modality-specific frequency and sensitivity signals are combined into an expert importance score, and an integer linear programming formulation assigns per-expert precision under a target bit budget.","experiments":"The evaluation covers three MoE-MLLM families, ten multimodal benchmarks, and several low-bit weight-only quantization settings. For example, experts frequently selected by text tokens may still fall below the layer-average total frequency because visual routing dominates. At W3A16, MODE reports only 2.84% average accuracy degradation on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct and 2.08% on Kimi-VL-A3B-Instruct. plot-beat 的 Plot-Beat F1（由独立 judge ensemble 抽取），并配套人物声线一致性、世界规则违背率、伏笔回收率、事件链时序一致性、主题复现、情绪弧线对齐（DTW）、对白归因准确率、母题持久性等。\n方法部分（N-VSSM）：在 Mamba-2 8B 解码器上引入每个 scene 更新一次的叙事潜变量 z_t∈R^256。每到 scene 边界，用事件抽取器得到 Existing works have introduced benchmarks to evaluate whether LLM agents can assist with reproducibility, but they are difficult to scale due to their reliance on substantial manual effort for data curation and evaluation. We introduce ReproRepo, a scalable framework for reproducibility evaluation that leverages human-raised GitHub issues as naturally occurring supervision on realistic reproduction bloc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:58:05Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18237.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是“如何大规模、低人工成本地评估LLM agent对科研复现问题的发现能力”。以往复现评测要么靠专家手工设计任务、注入错误、写rubric，要么样本量很小，而且常常只覆盖高质量artifact。作者想把这个评测过程做成可扩展、可持续更新、且更贴近真实研究实践的基准：给定论文和代码仓库，让agent通过静态审查找出真实用户在GitHub issue里报告过的复现阻塞点。","importance":"它不是预训练方法论文，但属于应当保留的eval类工作，因为它提供了一种新的、规模足够大的agent/research automation评测资产。对基座研究的启发主要有两点。第一，它把“真实用户遇到的问题”当监督信号，而不是专家合成任务，这种数据构造思路对训练数据和评测数据都很有借鉴意义。第二，它揭示了当前强agent在静态代码审查上的能力边界：语义层面能找到大部分问题区域，但精确定位和隐蔽错误仍然困难。对于做代码模型、agentic coding、自动科研、以及repo-level pretraining/eval的人，这个基准有实际参考价值。贡献量级主要在benchmark construction和evaluation protocol，而不是方法创新。","technical_approach":"框架名为ReproRepo，核心设计是用GitHub issues替代昂贵的专家标注。整体流程分三步。\n\n第一步是数据构造。作者从顶会论文中收集paper-repo对，再抓取对应仓库中的issue。随后筛选出与复现阻塞相关的issue，而不是一般功能请求或讨论。为了保证评测时agent不能直接“看到答案”，作者基于issue发生时间和修复PR时间构建仓库快照，例如issue-time snapshot、修复前snapshot、修复后snapshot。这样可以把真实历史问题转成隐藏测试项。\n\n第二步是agent任务定义。给agent论文和固定版本代码仓库，但不允许执行代码，只做compute-light的静态审查。agent需要输出可能的复现阻塞点。这个设置很重要，因为现代ML仓库往往依赖GPU、大数据集、外部API，完全执行成本太高；作者因此把问题限定为“静态审计能发现多少真实阻塞”。\n\n第三步是评测协议。作者把agent输出与人类issue进行匹配，既看exact match，也看semantic match；既看issue-level指标，也看paper-level指标，还统计成本。这个多层评估设计比较合理，因为复现问题往往存在表述差异，单纯exact match会低估agent能力，而只看语义相近又会高估其定位精度。论文还区分visible failures和less visible failures，帮助分析agent到底擅长发现哪类问题。\n\n与已有基准相比，ReproRepo的差异不在于任务形式多新，而在于监督来源和规模。它不依赖专家写题，而是直接利用自然发生的用户痛点；规模上覆盖1149篇论文、7553个issue，明显大于多数既有复现评测。代价是标签噪声和issue质量不可控，但作者用多种匹配指标和快照构造来缓解这一点。","experiments":"实验规模是这篇工作的主要亮点。作者在3个主要机器学习会议上构建了1149篇论文、7553个人类报告复现阻塞点的数据集，并评估了Claude Code、Codex与4个frontier LLM的组合。在静态审查设定下，结果比直觉上更强：Codex + GPT-5.5在NeurIPS 2024 main conference上，能为93.3%的论文找出至少一个语义相关的隐藏issue；在ICLR 2026上这一比例为89.7%。这说明即便不运行代码，强agent也能从论文与仓库结构中恢复相当多的人类复现痛点。\n\n但作者没有把这个结果解读成“复现问题已被解决”。更关键的发现是：agent的semantic coverage高，但exact localization仍弱。它们更擅长发现可见的失败模式，比如缺失依赖、脚本错误、明显配置不一致；对隐蔽错误、晚期崩溃、语义更复杂的问题，命中率明显差。论文还指出false positive rate较低，这意味着agent不是靠大量泛泛而谈的猜测刷高召回，而是确实能定位到相近问题区域。\n\n如果精读实验部分，建议重点看四类结果：一是不同模型/agent框架的paper-level与issue-level对比；二是exact match与semantic match之间的差距，这直接反映“知道哪里不对”和“准确指出哪一行/哪一模块不对”之间的落差；三是visible vs. less visible failure的分层分析；四是成本分析，因为这个benchmark的一个卖点就是可扩展性和更新便利性。","reading_guide":"这篇适合按“数据构造—评测协议—误差分析”的顺序读。\n\n先看Figure 1和Table snapshot如何对齐issue时间、如何处理修复前后版本，以及哪些issue被排除。这个部分决定benchmark是否真的在测复现阻塞，而不是在测仓库维护噪声。\n\n接着看evaluation metrics。尤其注意exact match和semantic match的定义、issue-level与paper-level指标的关系、以及false positive如何统计。很多agent benchmark最后争议都出在匹配规则，这篇也不例外。\n\n最后看qualitative analysis。最值得读的是失败案例：agent为什么能找到“语义区域”却不能精确定位；哪些问题静态分析天然难；是否存在从论文文本中猜到大致问题、但无法落实到代码位置的情况。若只想快速了解，读引言、Figure 1、Table The paper separates an agent harness from a system harness. An agent harness is a model in a tool-use loop for a single task. A system harness sits outside it: it converts higher-level goals into tasks, dispatches one or more agent harnesses, manages the repository and runtime environment, curates context, and routes feedback. The system is decomposed into five parts: tasks, agent harnesses, environment, context, and feedback signals. Feedback is further divided into inner-loop signals such as tests, type checks, linting, and compilation; middle-loop signals such as review, simulation, and maintainability checks; and outer-loop signals such as PR acceptance, reverts, incidents, and user outcomes. The proposed benchmark direction is to expose component-level measurements and grade against independent behavioral specifications rather than a single reference solution.","experiments":"There are no controlled experiments or new benchmark numbers. We find that, across diverse agentic and inter","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T05:42:19Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17541.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 agent 轨迹离线评测中的信息损失问题。许多 benchmark 只看最终是否成功，把多步交互过程压成一个二值结果，导致部分进展、求解速度、失败前接近成功的程度都被丢掉。结果是大量 instance-level 比较变成平局，评测集的有效样本量下降，系统之间更难区分。","importance":"对预训练本身是间接相关，但对 agent、RL post-training、交互式评测有实际启发。它提醒一个关键问题：所谓 benchmark saturation 有时不是模型真的不可区分，而是 metric 把可区分信号压没了。对于用 agent benchmark 评估推理模型、代码模型、工具使用模型的团队，这类轨迹级评测能提高统计效率，减少为了区分模型而盲目扩大评测集的需求。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 preference-based trajectory evaluation。论文不再先把每条轨迹压成 success rate 或 discounted return，而是直接比较两条轨迹的时间序列表现。其基本原则是 temporal preference：在达到相同任务进展的情况下，更早达到的轨迹更好。这样可以利用 trajectory 中的 progress profile 和 time-to-return profile，而不是只看终点。相较于常见的时间折扣方法，它强调不引入额外超参数，也不要求预先设定折扣系数；相较于只比较 partial return 的方法，它保留了“什么时候达到某个进展”的信息。","experiments":"实验覆盖经典强化学习环境和较新的 agentic / interactive benchmarks。论文报告的核心数字是：使用标准 success-based metric 时，instance-level comparison 平均约 75% 是平局；即使改用 partial return，平局率仍约 50%；轨迹偏好方法把平局率降到约 35%。这一变化带来更好的区分能力、排名稳定性和数据效率。论文还做了一个现象性统计：NeurIPS Datasets and Benchmarks track 论文摘要中提到 binary metrics 的比例从 2022 年的 5% 增至 2025 年的 18%；EMNLP 中对应比例从 9% 增至 21%，说明二值评测的使用还在增加。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。先看 Introduction 中 Figure 1 对 success metric 信息损失的说明，再看方法部分如何定义 temporal preference 和 trajectory-level comparison。实验部分重点看 tie rate、ranking stability、data efficiency 相关表格和图。对做模型预训练的人，最有用的不是具体公式，而是评测设计上的提醒：当模型越来越强时，metric 的分辨率会直接影响训练信号和模型选择。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses information loss in offline evaluation of agentic systems. Standard metrics often reduce a multi-step trajectory to a binary terminal success label, discarding partial progress, speed of progress, and near-miss behavior. This creates many tied comparisons and lowers the effective sample size of an evaluation set.","importance":"The connection to base pretraining is indirect, but the paper is useful for evaluating reasoning agents, tool-using models, and RL/post-training systems. Its main lesson is that apparent benchmark saturation can be caused by metric design rather than by true indistinguishability between systems. For expensive agent benchmarks, trajectory-aware evaluation can improve statistical power without simply increasing the number of test instances.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is preference-based trajectory evaluation. Instead of computing a scalar success rate or discounted return first, it compares trajectories directly through their progress over time. The guiding rule is temporal preference: if two trajectories reach the same level of task progress, the one that reaches it earlier is preferred. This preserves both progress profiles and time-to-return profiles. Unlike discounting-based metrics, the method is presented as avoiding additional discount hyperparameters; unlike partial-return comparisons, it keeps timing information rather than only the amount of progress.","experiments":"The evaluation spans both classic reinforcement-learning environments and modern agentic or interactive benchmarks. The central empirical result is that success-based metrics yield tied instance-level comparisons on roughly 75% of cases. Partial-return comparisons still tie on about 50%. Trajectory-aware preferences reduce the tie rate to about 35%, with corresponding gains in discriminative power, ranking stability, and data efficiency. The paper also reports an adoption trend: mentions of binary metrics in NeurIPS Datasets and Benchmarks abstracts increased from 5% in 2022 to 18% in 2025, and from 9% to 21% at EMNLP over the same period.","reading_guide":"A skim is sufficient, around 20–30 minutes. In the experiments, focus on tie rates, ranking stability, and data-efficiency plots or tables. The main takeaway for pretraining-oriented researchers is about evaluation resolution: when models become stronger, the metric can become the bottleneck in model selection and training-signal quality.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"指出了 Agent 评测中 binary success rate 导致的信息丢失问题，提出的 trajectory preference 视角对设计更 robust 的 eval metric 有启发，建议看 §Method 和 Fig 1。","en":"Points out the information loss caused by binary success rates in Agent evaluation; the proposed trajectory preference perspective is inspiring for designing more robust eval metrics. with","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T03:26:15Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17464.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是“如何设计更强的 membership inference attack”，而是更基础的问题：现有 LLM MIA 评测基准往往把成员集和非成员集做出了可被数据分布本身区分的偏差，导致攻击效果主要来自 dataset shift，而不是模型是否记住了训练样本。论文要解决的是，怎样构造一个统计上更干净的 MIA benchmark，使攻击分数真正反映预训练模型的成员泄露，而不是时间切分、去重方式或语料来源差异带来的伪信号。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它直接影响对隐私泄露、memorization、unlearning 成效的判断，因为这些问题都常借 MIA 当代理指标；如果 benchmark 这类时间切分，成员和非成员来自不同时间段，语言风格、主题分布、实体频率都会漂移；另一类是基于开源训练/验证切分的做法，尤其在训练集做过去重时，train 与 val 的重复度结构不同，member / non-member 会因 dedup 机制而天然可分。作者据此构造更干净的 split，关键思想是利用“固定训练时点前后样本来自同一上游分布”的条件，尽量让成员与非成员只在是否被当前 checkpoint 见过这一点上不同，而不引入额外来源差异。然后用 blind baselines 做 sanity check：这些基线不访问目标模型，只基于文本本身训练简单分类器或统计判别器；如果 blind 方法已经能高精度区分 member / non-member，说明 split 有问题，不能用于评估 MIA。完成 split 验证后，作者再在两个常见开源 LLM family 上系统比较一组已有 MIA 方法，报告 AUC 和低 FPR 下的 TPR。技术上最有价值的部分不是某个攻击器结构，而是把 benchmark 设计中的 i.i.d. 假设、去重诱导偏差、时间漂移偏差逐一形式化并落实到可操作的评测流程。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容可以确认几项关键实验结论。第一，作者复现并强调了旧 benchmark 的问题：在 WikiMIA 一类时间切分设置上，简单 bag-of-words blind classifier 在 FPR=5% 时可达到 94.7% 的 TPR，明显说明攻击成功主要来自分布差异，而不是模型泄露。第二，作者在新构造的 clean split 上先做 blind baseline sanity check，目标是让这些不访问模型的方法接近随机水平，否则 benchmark 不成立。第三，在通过 sanity check 的 split 上，常见 MIA 方法的表现比旧文献里“看起来很高”的结果要有限得多，说明过去不少结论被 benchmark 污染放大了。由于提供的全文片段未包含完整结果表、模型家族名称和全部具体数值，无法可靠补充更多参数规模、AUC/TPR 细节；但从论文结构看，最值得看的应是不同 split 构造下 blind baseline 与 model-aware attack dedup、val-against-train dedup 为什么都会破坏 member / non-member 同分布假设的论证。实验部分优先看三类结果：1）旧 benchmark 上 blind baseline 的异常高分；2）新 split 上 blind baseline 是否失效；3）新 split 上已有 MIA 方法还剩多少真实能力。若只花 20–30 分钟，读清这三点就够了。对做预训练数据、去重、数据审计的人，值得进一步深挖作者对 dedup-induced distribution shift 的分析，因为这和训练语料构造直接相关。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a foundational issue in membership inference for LLMs: many existing benchmarks do not isolate membership signal from dataset artifacts. Member and non-member sets are often separable because of temporal drift, source mismatch, or asymmetric deduplication, so reported attack performance reflects distribution shift rather than whether the model actually encoded training examples. The core problem is how to build a statistically clean MIA benchmark where success must come from model-dependent leakage.","importance":"Its value is methodological rather than algorithmic. For pretraining research, MIA is often used as a proxy for privacy leakage, memorization, and even unlearning efficacy. If the benchmark is contaminated, all three are mismeasured. The paper also clarifies an important data-engineering point: deduplication is not neutral for evaluation. Deduplicating only train, or deduplicating validation against train, changes the marginal distribution of member vs. non-member examples and can create artificial separability. The proposed sanity-check recipe—test every split with blind baselines before evaluating model-aware attacks—should be treated as a minimum standard for future privacy and data-attribution work.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"Several key findings are clear from the full text excerpt. First, the paper highlights how flawed prior benchmarks can be: on a temporal-cutoff setup like WikiMIA, a simple bag-of-words blind classifier reaches 94.7% TPR at 5% FPR, showing that the benchmark is dominated by distributional artifacts. Second, on the newly constructed clean splits, blind baselines are used as a gatekeeping test and are expected to fall near chance; otherwise the split is rejected. Third, once evaluated on these cleaner splits, standard MIA methods show much more limited performance than prior literature suggested. The provided text does not include the full result tables, exact model families, or all numerical comparisons, so it would be unsafe to invent detailed AUC values or parameter counts. The most informative experimental comparisons are likely: flawed vs. clean splits, blind vs. model-aware attacks, and the effect of different deduplication choices on attack performance.","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the benchmark critique. The key question is not which attack wins, but whether the evaluation setup is valid at all. Then read the section explaining why temporal cutoffs and asymmetric deduplication violate the same-distribution assumption between members and non-members. In this work, we systematically analyze modality imbalance in L","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-15T21:05:57Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17296.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注统一多模态模型在 LoRA 指令微调阶段的模态失衡问题。更具体地说，在同时做文本生成和图像生成时，共享参数上的语言梯度往往显著强于视觉梯度，导致模型在语言侧保留较好能力，但图像生成质量下降更明显。作者要解决的不是一般意义上的多任务冲突，而是在统一 AR 多模态模型、尤其是参数高效微调场景下，如何避免弱模态在优化中被强模态持续压制。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更接近 pretrain 到 post-train 接口的问题，但启发是明确的。统一多模态模型常把“共享 backbone + 统一 token 序列”视为自然设计，然而一旦进入 instruction tuning，优化信号并不天然平衡。论文给出的价值在于把这种失衡从经验现象落到梯度层面，并说明简单调 loss 权重往往不够，因为主导模态、失衡程度会随任务、层和训练阶段变化。对做原生多模态预训练的人来说，这提示了两件事：第一，后训练阶段的梯度结构会反向暴露预训练表示是否真正均衡；第二，如果统一模型在微调时持续出现模态压制，那么预训练目标、tokenizer 设计、loss 归一化和参数分配可能都需要重新审视。量级上，这是一个扎实的优化修补工作，不是新的统一建模范式。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Pareto LoRA。作者把文本损失和图像损失视为两个耦合目标，不再直接用固定系数把它们线性相加，而是在每一步 LoRA 更新时，寻找一个对两者都成立的共同下降方向。形式上，设共享可训练参数上的两组梯度分别为 g_txt 和 g_img，目标是在它们的凸包里找最小范数向量；按照 MGDA 的结论，这个向量要么对应 Pareto stationary point，要么给出同时改善两个目标的下降方向。由于这里只有两个目标，最优组合系数可以闭式求解，因此不会引入很重的优化开销。\n\n论文并不是机械地每步都做 Pareto 融合，而是先诊断梯度关系。作者使用两个指标：梯度余弦相似度衡量方向冲突，梯度范数比衡量谁在主导更新。这样做的理由很合理：如果两模态梯度本来方向一致、量级也接近，就没有必要强行干预；真正需要处理的是“方向冲突”或“量级悬殊”导致的压制。这个设计比单纯 loss reweighting 更细，因为 loss 权重只能在标量层面调节，而无法处理不同层、不同阶段、不同 batch 上梯度几何关系的变化。\n\n从 LoRA 视角看，这篇的针对性也比较强。LoRA 只更新低秩增量参数，容量有限，因此一旦共享更新方向长期被语言模态牵引，视觉侧更容易吃亏。Pareto LoRA 等于在有限适配容量下，尽量把每一步更新投到一个更平衡的方向上。它与全参数微调、多任务梯度手术方法的区别在于：目标是统一多模态生成，作用对象是 LoRA 参数，且重点是文本-图像两模态的平衡而不是泛化到任意多任务。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容可以确认几类关键实验结论。第一，作者做了一个很重要的对照：把统一多模态模型与对应的单模态模型分别在同一数据上微调，结果显示视觉侧相对单模态基线的退化明显大于文本侧，说明失衡不是抽象猜测，而是可测的性能差异。第二，作者直接分析了共享参数上的模态梯度，发现四个任务里有三个存在显著梯度失衡，而且梯度量级差异可以达到数量级级别。第三，失衡方向和严重程度会随任务、层和训练阶段变化，这也是固定 α 权重难以奏效的实证依据。\n\n由于当前提供的正文片段没有完整结果表，无法可靠写出具体模型规模、数据集名称、图像生成指标和文本指标的绝对数值，也无法确认 Pareto LoRA 相比 vanilla LoRA、loss reweighting 或其他 gradient balancing 方法的精确提升幅度。精读全文时，最值得抓的数字有四类：1）文本与图像相对单模态基线的性能跌幅；2）不同层上的梯度范数比统计；3）Pareto LoRA 在 interleaved generation 任务上的图像质量提升是否伴随文本能力保持；4）额外计算开销和训练稳定性。\n\n如果论文做了消融，建议重点看：只做 magnitude balancing、只做 conflict handling、以及完整 Pareto 方案三者的差异；还有在不同 rank 的 LoRA 下，方法收益是否更明显。若收益主要集中在低 rank 场景，那就说明它确实是在缓解参数高效微调的容量瓶颈，而不只是一般优化噪声。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读。先看引言和问题分析部分，确认作者如何证明“视觉是被压制的弱模态”，尤其是单模态对照实验和梯度统计图。然后看方法部分关于 bi-objective optimization、MGDA 闭式解、以及何时触发 Pareto 融合的策略。这里决定方法是否只是把现有多目标优化套到多模态 LoRA 上，还是做了针对性设计。\n\n实验部分优先看 Figure 2、Figure 3、Figure 4 这类问题诊断图，再看主结果表和消融。若时间有限，20 分钟左右可以完成主要信息提取。真正值得思考的不是“这个方法能否普遍替代 LoRA”，而是它揭示了统一多模态模型在后训练阶段的一个结构性问题：共享参数并不等于共享受益，尤其当 token 分布、loss 尺度和生成难度差异很大时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies modality imbalance during LoRA-based instruction tuning of unified multimodal models. In interleaved text-image generation, gradients from the language side often dominate the shared parameters, so text capability is preserved much better than image generation quality. The target problem is therefore not generic multi-task interference, but a specific optimization failure mode in unified autoregressive multimodal models under parameter-efficient adaptation: the weaker modality is consistently suppressed by the stronger one.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface. Unified multimodal models often assume that a shared backbone and a unified token space are enough to support balanced adaptation. The paper shows that this is not true at the gradient level. Its main value is diagnostic as much as algorithmic: simple loss reweighting is often insufficient because both the dominant modality and the severity of imbalance vary across tasks, layers, and training stages. For researchers working on native multimodal pretraining, this is a useful signal. If instruction tuning repeatedly suppresses one modality, then the issue may trace back to pretraining objectives, tokenizer design, loss normalization, or parameter allocation. The contribution is best viewed as a careful optimization fix rather than a new modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the problem-analysis section rather than the final results. The most useful material is the evidence that vision is the suppressed modality: the unimodal-versus-unified comparison and the gradient statistics. Then read the method section for the bi-objective formulation, the closed-form MGDA solution, and the rule for when Pareto integration is actually applied.\n\nIn the experiments, prioritize the diagnostic figures first—especially the ones showing performance gaps and gradient imbalance across tasks or layers—then read the main result table and ablations. A 20-minute pass is enough to capture the main idea. Video-LLMs: deciding when to respond in a continuous stream, preserving useful context over long horizons, and training/evaluating models for realistic streaming settings. The authors argue that many prior systems formulate silence as an EOS prediction problem, which creates severe class imbalance, contradictory supervision on adjacent frames, mismatch with semantic pretraining objectives, and contamination of the token distribution by an overused control token. At the same time, standard sliding-window memory quickly forgets distant events, making long-horizon questions hard or impossible to answer. The goal is therefore not offline video QA, but an always-on streaming assistant that can proactively respond at the right time and retrieve relevant history over long durations.","importance":"Its relevance to core LLM pretraining is limited, because the paper is mainly about online video understanding and inference-time system design rather than pretraining objectives, data mixtures, or optimization dynamics. That is a broader lesson about alignment between training targets and the model’s native generative distribution, especially for multimodal systems with control signals. Second, the hierarchical memory design is an inference-side solution, but it points to a real open question for long-video and long-audio foundation models: how to expose an explicit memory interface instead of relying only on larger context windows. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the generat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:59:22Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18249.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"统一多模态模型想做到“同一上下文里既能理解也能生成”，但主流做法往往用两套视觉 tokenizer（理解/生成各一套），导致生成出来的图像 token 不能被模型直接当作“可理解的上下文”使用，必须再走一次重编码，破坏 shared-context。论文要解决的是：能否用单一离散视觉 tokenizer 同时满足理解所需的语义抽象与生成所需的细节保真，并且在 AR next-token 框架下把视觉 token 的词表规模和序列长度做得足够可训练、可扩展。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发点在 tokenizer 与统一建模接口：\n1) 把“理解/生成分裂”归因到表示空间割裂，而不是简单的 loss 或解码器选择，强调 tokenizer 是 unified multimodal pretrain 的关键瓶颈之一；\n2) 用 lookup-free 的 bitwise 离散化把视觉词表规模从“码本大小”约束中解放出来，提供一种不同于 VQ codebook 的扩展路径；\n3) 训练上把视觉 tokenizer/decoder 冻结，把主要学习压力放在 AR 主干上，属于一种更接近“固定感知前端 + 统一 LM”的 recipe，便于稳定大规模预训练与后续对齐（SFT/RL）。\n局限也明确：bitwise token 的可学习语义结构、以及“理论词表指数级”与“有效可用词表”的差距，需要看全文实验是否给出足够证据。","technical_approach":"UniAR 的核心是“单 tokenizer + AR 统一建模”。关键组件：\n1) 多层特征融合的视觉 tokenizer：从预训练 ViT/视觉编码器抽取多层特征，浅层保细节（纹理/颜色/边缘），深层保语义（物体/布局），融合后再离散化，试图同时服务生成与理解。相比只用最后一层做离散 token（更偏语义）或只做重建友好的低层特征（更偏细节），它把两类需求显式拼到同一 token 表示里。\n2) lookup-free bitwise quantization：把连续特征映射为二进制向量（如 64-bit），不需要显式 codebook 查表；理论上 2^b 的组合数带来巨大词表容量。与 VQ-VAE/ViT-VQ 的差异在于：不学习离散码本，而是学习到二值化边界/投影，使离散码由 bit 组合决定。\n3) 并行 bitwise 预测 + 2×2 网格：在 AR next-token 框架下，不是逐 bit 生成，而是在每个 2×2 spatial grid 内并行预测多级二进制向量，从而显著缩短视觉序列长度；论文声称达到 32× 的视觉压缩比。\n4) DiT-based 视觉解码器 + 分辨率 upsampling：解码器只条件在视觉 token 上（不再额外喂文本 prompt），并通过 upsampling 支持高分辨率；文中给出 1024×1024 只需预测 256 个视觉 token 的设定。\n5) 训练流程：三阶段（大规模多模态预训练 → 高质量数据 SFT → RL 提升生成），且 tokenizer 与 decoder 全程冻结；decoder 只在 RL 阶段用于把 token 解码成图像以计算 reward。这一点等价于把“视觉离散空间”当作固定通信协议，减少端到端联训的不稳定与漂移风险。\n与相关工作对比：作者点名 Infinity（同样用二值量化）但强调其面向纯生成与重建优化；UniAR 目标是统一理解+生成，并采用标准 next-token（而非 next-scale）范式。","experiments":"给定的全文片段没有包含可核验的完整实验表格与具体数值（如模型规模、预训练 token 数、各 benchmark 分数、消融提升幅度）。目前只能从引言提取作者主张：\n- 生成侧：在“文本渲染（text context. Most existing systems still rely on two different visual tokenizers (one for understanding, one for generation), which splits the representation space: images generated as tokens cannot be directly re-consumed for understanding without re-encoding. This paper targets a single discrete visual tokenizer that simultaneously preserves high-level semantics (for understanding) and low-level details (for generation), while keeping vocabulary scaling and AR sequence length practical under next-token training.","importance":"The main pretraining-facing contribution is a tokenizer-centric unification recipe:\n- It frames the “not truly unified” issue as a representation-space mismatch caused by dual tokenizers, not merely a decoder or loss choice.\n- It proposes lookup-free bitwise discretization as an alternative scaling path to VQ codebooks, decoupling vocabulary capacity from explicit codebook size.\n- It adopts a stable training interface (freeze tokenizer/decoder; train the AR backbone; introduce the decoder only for RL reward computation), which is aligned with large-scale pretrain + post-train pipelines.\nCaveat: the gap between theoretical 2^b code capacity and the effective, learnable code usage needs to be validated by the full experimental section.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the main quantitative tables or exact benchmark numbers (model size, data scale, metric deltas, ablations). Qwen-Rob","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T12:14:39Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17846.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.RO","cs.CV","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"机器人操作（manipulation）想复用“互联网多源数据对齐→规模化→涌现泛化”的 表示，并用按维度的 binary masking 兼容不同机器人形态（缺失的关节/末端执行器维度用 mask 处理）。\n- Motion alignment：用 camera-frame 的 delta pose 参数化，让视觉上相似的运动在数值上更接近，减少坐标系差异带来的分布裂缝。\n- Behavior alignment：用 system prompt + in-context policy adaptation，把同一 episode 的执行历史作为隐式 embodiment 标识，促使策略做运动学相关的自适应。\n- Dual-stream co-training：同时在 manipulation 数据与 curated vision-language 流上训练，避免 VLM 主干在学动作时“变笨”。\n- 数据规模化：human-to-robot synthesis 把第一视角手部演示转成 15 种平台的机器人轨迹；再做多源数据清洗/统一，累计约 38,100 小时。\n这些设计更像“把异构监督信号对齐到统一 token/向量接口”的工程化系统方案，而不是提出新的语言建模目标。","experiments":"片段信息显示其评测维度包括 instruction following、cross-embodiment transfer、in-distribution performance，以及 OOD 设置（如 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin-...）。但当前内容未给出可核验的具体成功率/提升幅度/模型规模曲线。\n可确认的量化信息：训练数据规模约 38,100 小时，来源为开源机器人数据集 + 第一视角人类视频 + 合成数据；覆盖 15 种平台的人到机器人合成。\n若要判断其“scaling law / emergent generalization”是否站得住，需要全文中的：1) 随数据量/模型量增长的对数线性或幂律拟合；2) 对齐模块的消融（去掉 motion/representation/behavior alignment 的退化幅度）；3) 双流共训 vs 只训操作数据的遗忘对比。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看 recipe (align heterogeneous data → scale training → generalization emerges), but manipulation data is inherently heterogeneous across embodiments and expensive, with narrow diversity. The paper addresses how to align representations, motions, and behaviors across different robots so that adding more data yields synergy rather than gradient interference, and then tests whether generalization improves with scale.","importance":"Relevance to core LLM pretraining is indirect (this is primarily a VLA/robotics foundation model report), but it offers two transferable pretraining lessons:\n- Alignment as a prerequisite for scaling: when sources are numerically incompatible (coordinate frames, action parameterizations), more data can hurt. We refer to this behavior as overthinking. We study this phenomenon from the perspective of GRPO-style reinforcement learning (RL) post-training, framing it as a training-time credit-assignment problem rather than merely a decoding-time stopping problem. In rollouts sampled at the onset of GRPO training, we observe that ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T13:10:30Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17890.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 GRPO 这类序列级奖励的 RL post-train 中，模型一旦在 chain-of-thought 里“已经出现可验证正确答案”，后续继续生成冗余推理（overthinking）会被和“到达正确答案的前缀”一起获得正向 credit，从而被训练过程系统性强化。论文要解决的不是解码时怎么停，而是训练时的 credit assignment：如何在不伤害到达正确答案的推理前缀的前提下，削弱成功轨迹中答案出现之后那段冗余 token 的偏好信号。","importance":"把“过度思考”从 decoding/长度控制问题，改写成 GRPO/序列级优势函数带来的结构性训练偏差，并给出一个可落地的训练期干预。对推理模型的 post-train recipe 有直接影响：如果你的 verifier 能定位答案出现点，DRE 这类编辑式偏好学习可以在基本不掉准确率的情况下显著降长度与算力成本，并减少训练后期形成的“反复检查/重复验证/答案回看”模式。更重要的是，它提供了一个通用视角：当 reward 只在序列末端给出且更新对整段轨迹同权时，任何“成功后仍继续生成”的行为都可能被错误归因并固化。","technical_approach":"核心做法是 Dynamic Rollout Editing (DRE)：\n1) 让“答案出现”可观测：对每条 rollout，用 parser 抽取中间答案候选，并用外部 verifier 判定正确性，定义最早可验证正确答案出现的位置作为 answer emergence point。这样能把轨迹分成“到达答案的前缀”和“答案后冗余段”。\n2) 训练期编辑成功轨迹：当一条成功轨迹在 answer emergence 之后仍有明显 overthinking，构造一个 edited counterpart：保留从开头到一个保守边界（围绕 emergence 点，避免误删必要 token）的 verified prefix，把后续 overthinking 替换/截断为更短的版本（目标是保留正确答案并去掉冗余）。\n3) 在同一个 GRPO group 内做相对偏好：把原成功轨迹与 edited 轨迹放在同组比较，让 edited 轨迹获得更高 preference/优势，从而把“成功”的正向信号更多分配给短轨迹，弱化‘成功=长且反复检查’的耦合。\n4) 两个训练细节用于避免误伤前缀并稳定学习信号：\n- Prefix masking：对原轨迹与 edited 轨迹共享的 verified prefix 不施加负向更新，避免因为偏好 edited 而把到达答案所需的前缀也压下去。\n- GClip：在编辑点附近给更可控的学习信号（论文将其作为对 edited tokens/边界处梯度的稳定化手段），避免“硬截断”导致的局部不连续训练效应。\n与常见替代方案的差异：\n- 不是 length penalty：长度惩罚会同时压制有用推理与无用推理，且对不同题型的最优长度不一致。\n- 不是 stopping controller/early-exit：那些方法在策略学成后再控长度；DRE 直接改训练信号，阻断反馈回路。\n- 不是对整条成功轨迹降权：那会削弱到达答案的关键前缀；DRE 通过“保前缀、改后缀”的编辑把 credit 分离。","experiments":"全文宣称覆盖数学推理、科学 QA、代码生成等任务，结论集中在两点：\n- 长度主要在 answer emergence 之后被削减：DRE 的长度收益不是靠缩短必要推理，而是减少答案出现后的冗余段（反复检查、重复验证、答案回看）。\n- 性能基本保持：在保持任务成功率/准确率接近 vanilla GRPO 的同时，显著降低平均生成长度与推理成本，并在不同任务上复现。\n给出的机制性证据是：GRPO 训练早期，同一 prompt 下成功轨迹在答案出现后往往比失败轨迹更“长一点”，这个微小偏差在序列级优势更新下会被放大，形成 overthinking 的自增强回路；DRE 通过组内偏好把这个回路切断。\n（注：你提供的全文片段未包含具体表格数字、模型规模、数据量与提升幅度；若需要精确数值，需要对照论文的实验表与附录。）","reading_guide":"精读优先级：\n- 先看“Overthinking After verifiably correct answer and (ii) redundant tokens generated after the answer has already emerged. Because GRPO assigns the same positive advantage to the whole successful trajectory, it cannot separate credit between these two parts, and it may systematically reinforce “overthinking after answer emergence”. The paper targets this as a training-time credit-assignment issue rather than a decoding-time stopping problem.","importance":"The main contribution is a concrete diagnosis of why overthinking can get amplified during GRPO training, plus a training-time intervention that reduces post-answer verbosity without sacrificing task success. Practically, if you can reliably detect the earliest verifiable answer point (via parsing + external verification), the proposed editing-based preference signal can lower inference cost and curb common failure patterns like repeated checking and answer revisiting. Conceptually, it highlights a broader RLHF/RL-posttrain pitfall: whenever reward is terminal and updates are trajectory-level, any behavior that co-occurs with success after the goal is achieved can be inadvertently reinforced.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"The paper reports consistent gains across math reasoning, science QA, and code generation: generation length is reduced primarily after the answer emergence point, while task performance remains close to vanilla GRPO. pr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T08:52:15Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17687.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何让 Large Reasoning Models 在不牺牲甚至提升正确率的前提下，自动把 CoT 推理长度压到“刚好够用”，避免对简单题也输出冗长推理带来的算力与时延浪费；并给出一个可操作的“推理何时足够”的判据，而不是依赖离散档位/手工预算。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接贡献（更偏 post-train / test-time compute 控制），但对“推理 token 作为可控资源”的训练信号设计有参考价值：1）把“足够性”形式化为可学习的连续控制目标，替代常见的 on/off 或少数档位；2）给出一个从现有长 CoT 轨迹中挖出短监督信号（MSC）的数据构造思路，属于一种面向推理效率的蒸馏/重标注；3）提出同时惩罚不足与过度推理的 reward 形状，和训练中分布漂移（reasoning length 分布变化）对应的动态采样池设计。对做 Sθ(z|x,y*)。论文用答案在条件 (x,z) 下的似然来度量支持度：从联合概率转为几何平均（per-token average）以避免长答案导致的指数衰减不稳定。然后用阈值 δ 判定“δ-sufficient”，并在句子级别找最短前缀作为 MSC（避免 token 级截断破坏推理结构）。\n\n在此基础上提出 SuCo 两阶段训练：\n1）Stage I：MSC-Aligned Fine-Tuning (MFT)。先用已有“全量 CoT”轨迹为每个样本抽取 MSC 前缀，构造 (x, MSC, y*) 的监督数据，再做 SFT，让模型内化“短但够用”的推理风格。这里的关键不是再写一个短 CoT 模板，而是用 sufficiency 判据从长轨迹中自动挖出最短可用前缀，属于一种基于模型置信度的重标注。\n2）Stage II：Sufficiency-Aware Policy Optimization (SAPO)。用 RL 进一步学会按题目难度连续调节推理长度。论文强调两点：其一是 reward 既惩罚推理不足（导致答错或低 sufficiency），也惩罚过度推理（冗余 token）；其二是维护一个 dynamic complexity pool 跟踪训练过程中推理长度/难度分布的变化，用于稳定采样与避免策略更新后分布漂移导致的训练不稳。\n\n与已有 ALRM 的差异：不依赖外部分类器/域标签来选模式，也不把预算离散成几档；而是用“足够性阈值 + 最短前缀”把控制目标连续化，并把监督信号从长 CoT 自动提炼出来。","experiments":"全文声称在数学、代码、科学三类任务上、1.5B 与 7B 两个尺度评测；在 MATH 的 5 个难度等级上，MSC 相比 full CoT 同时减少推理 token 且准确率更高（论文在引言用 Figure 1 Existing OPSD methods are inherently autoregressive-centric. They inject privileged information via left-to-right prefix conditioning with token-level divergence supervision, a design that fundamentally conflicts with the arbitraryorder generation of dLLMs. We introduce d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework tailored for dLLMs. Our ap","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:24:57Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18195.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 diffusion LLM 的 on-policy self-distillation 如何定义的问题。AR 模型里的 OPSD 通常把 privileged information 放在 prefix 中，并做 token-level divergence；但 dLLM 是迭代去噪、任意顺序生成，这种左到右前缀条件和 token 级监督并不匹配。d-OPSD 的目标是在不引入更强外部 teacher 的情况下，让 dLLM 从自己的生成轨迹中获得更密集的自蒸馏信号。","importance":"它对新型语言建模范式的 post-train 接口有参考意义。dLLM 近期已经能用 RLVR 做 reasoning post-training，但 RLVR 的 outcome reward 稀疏，SFT 又有 off-policy 和 exposure bias 问题。d-OPSD 提供了一种更贴合去噪生成过程的训练信号：用模型自己生成的答案作为 suffix privileged information，再用 step-level divergence 监督整个去噪转移。这把 AR post-training 中的 OPSD 思路迁移到 diffusion LM，但没有照搬 token-level objective。对研究 diffusion LM 的训练目标、推理过程和 reasoning 能力提升有启发。","technical_approach":"核心有两处改动。第一，self-teacher 的构造不再把参考解或 privileged information 拼到 prompt 后面作为 prefix 条件，而是利用 dLLM 可做非自回归条件建模的特点，把模型自己生成的答案作为 suffix condition。这样 teacher 学到的是在已知“未来答案”时，对前面推理轨迹或中间内容的条件分布，可理解为从 self future-experience 中提供训练信号。第二，监督粒度从 token-level divergence 改为 step-level divergence。dLLM 每一步会对多个 masked tokens 同时预测，然后保留一部分、重新 mask 一部分；单个 token 的 next-token KL 并不能自然对应这个 Markov denoising transition。d-OPSD 因此在每个去噪 step 上比较 student transition 与 self-teacher transition，使蒸馏目标和 dLLM 的推理机制对齐。它和 RLVR 的区别是提供密集分布监督，而不是只依赖最终答案奖励；和 SFT 的区别是训练轨迹来自 student 自身，on-policy 程度更高。","experiments":"论文在四个 reasoning 任务上比较 d-OPSD、RLVR 和 SFT。给定内容显示，d-OPSD 在 reasoning performance 和 sample efficiency 上稳定优于这些 baseline。实验还分析了不同设置对效果的影响，用来支持两个设计选择：suffix privileged conditioning 比把 privileged information 作为前缀更适合 dLLM；step-level divergence 比 token-level 监督更符合迭代去噪。给定内容未包含任务名称、模型规模、训练样本量、步数和具体分数，因此需要在实验表中重点核对提升幅度，以及是否在不同 denoising steps、不同采样策略、不同自生成答案质量下保持稳定。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 AR-OPSD 与 dLLM 不匹配的两点分析：prefix conditioning 和 token-level divergence。然后看 Preliminaries，确认作者如何定义 dLLM 的 forward corruption、reverse denoising、masked token 保留与 remasking。方法部分重点看 self-teacher 的 suffix conditioning 形式，以及 step-level divergence 的具体计算对象。实验部分建议优先看四类结果：与 RLVR/SFT 的主结果，样本效率曲线，privileged information 放置方式的消融，token-level 与 step-level supervision 的消融。若要进一步判断价值，需要看它是否依赖高质量自生成答案筛选，以及额外推理成本是否会影响训练吞吐。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how on-policy self-distillation should be formulated for diffusion LLMs. OPSD methods for autoregressive models usually inject privileged information through left-to-right prefix conditioning and apply token-level divergence. That design does not fit dLLMs, which generate by iterative denoising and can condition on arbitrary parts of the sequence. d-OPSD aims to give a dLLM dense self-distillation signals without requiring a stronger external teacher.","importance":"The work is relevant to the interface between alternative language modeling objectives and post-training. Recent dLLMs can benefit from RLVR, but outcome rewards are sparse, while SFT uses off-policy trajectories and can suffer from exposure bias. d-OPSD proposes a training signal that matches the denoising process more closely: use the model’s own generated answer as suffix privileged information, then distill at the denoising-step level. This is a useful direction for studying how reasoning training should differ between autoregressive LMs and diffusion LMs.","technical_approach":"The method makes two main changes to AR-style OPSD. First, the self-teacher is built with suffix conditioning rather than prefix conditioning. Instead of appending a reference solution to the prompt in a left-to-right format, d-OPSD uses the model’s own generated answer as a future suffix condition. This exploits the bidirectional or arbitrary-order conditioning ability of dLLMs and keeps the data on-policy. Second, the divergence objective is moved from token level to step level. In a dLLM, each denoising step predicts many masked positions, commits to a subset, and may remask others. A next-token KL objective is therefore not the natural unit of supervision. d-OPSD compares the student and self-teacher denoising transitions at each step, aligning the distillation target with the Markov denoising process. Compared with RLVR, it provides dense distributional feedback. Compared with SFT, it trains on trajectories sampled from the student itself.","experiments":"The paper reports experiments on four reasoning tasks, comparing d-OPSD with RLVR and SFT baselines. The provided content states that d-OPSD improves both reasoning performance and sample efficiency. The ablations are designed to test the two central choices: suffix-based privileged conditioning versus prefix-style conditioning, and step-level divergence versus token-level supervision. Exact task names, model sizes, training budgets, and score deltas are not available in the supplied text, so the experimental tables should be checked for the actual margins and for robustness across denoising step counts, sampling strategies, and different qualities of self-generated answers.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim should cover the main contribution. Start with the Introduction, especially the two failure modes of directly applying AR-OPSD to dLLMs: prefix conditioning and token-level divergence. Then read the preliminaries to verify the paper’s notation for forward corruption, reverse denoising, token commitment, and remasking. In the method section, focus on the suffix-conditioned self-teacher and the exact definition of step-level divergence. In the experiments, prioritize the main comparison with RLVR and SFT, the sample-efficiency curves, and the ablations on privileged-information placement and supervision granularity. For a deeper assessment, check whether the method depends on filtering high-quality self-generated answers and how much extra computation the self-teacher pass adds.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看方法和 ablation 即可；它提醒 dLLM 后训练不能照搬 AR 的 prefix 教师信号。","en":"Skim the method and ablations; the useful point is that dLLM post-training cannot simply inherit AR prefix-based teacher signals."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yifu Luo","Zeyu Chen","Haoyu Wang","Xinhao Hu","Yuxuan Zhang","Zhizhou Sha","Shiwei Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","self-distillation","post-training","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18195","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.18195","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.18216","title":"Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization: Teacher in Prompts, Not Gradients","abstract":"Knowledge distillation transfers a teacher's competence to a small student but is brittle in the small-student regime: forcing the student to imitate logits from a much larger teacher concentrates it on the teacher's sharpest modes, hurting generalization on benchmark families beyond the training corpus. Reinforcement learning (RL) avoids logit imitation by training on the student's own rollouts. However, on questions where every rollout fails-yielding zero advantage and being silently discarded","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T17:46:02Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_18216.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很现实的问题：当学生模型很小，教师模型很强时，常见的两条知识迁移路线都不太稳。蒸馏的问题在于，小模型去拟合大模型的logit分布，容易被教师最尖锐的模式牵着走，训练集内能学到，分布外泛化却变脆；纯RL的问题在于，难题上如果学生所有rollout都失败，优势为零，这些样本就被训练过程直接丢掉，模型恰恰学不到自己最不会的部分。作者要解决的不是“如何把教师答案塞给学生”，而是“如何在不做logit imitation、也不把教师回答直接写进policy gradient的前提下，让小模型从强教师那里得到可学习的帮助，同时保持on-policy训练的稳定性”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不在于它是一个post-training技巧，而在于它触到了一个更底层的问题：小模型如何吸收强模型知识而不被其分布压塌。这个问题和小模型预训练、continual pretrain、RL-pretrain接口都有关。它的贡献不是提出一个全新的RL算法理论，而是给出了一种很工程化、但设计上相当克制的知识注入方式：把教师放进prompt，而不是放进梯度。这个选择的意义在于，它把“教师提供信息”和“学生对自己行为负责”拆开了。对于做小模型、端侧模型、蒸馏替代方案、以及RL与蒸馏混合训练的人，这个思路有参考价值。量级上，它更像是一个针对小模型RL训练病灶的有效recipe，而不是会改写大规模预训练主范式的工作。","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点不是大模型极限性能，而是小模型知识迁移和泛化。作者将ZPPO实例化在Qwen3.5系列小模型上，关注0.8B和2B这类容量受限学生。任务主要覆盖数学与推理类设置，并把训练集内收益和训练分布外benchmark泛化分开看。论文反复强调的结果有三类。\n\n第一，ZPPO相对蒸馏和常规RL，在小模型上更稳地提升分布外泛化。作者给出的核心论点是：蒸馏方法在训练语料附近能学到教师行为，但在benchmark groups：如果按普通RL流程直接丢弃，这部分难题几乎没有学习信号；而ZPPO通过prompt改写把它们重新变成可学样本。\n\n第三，训练recipe对小模型影响很大。文中指出，iterations per step、batch-level advantage normalization、以及归一化时排除zero-advantage groups，这些实现细节并不昂贵，但消融后性能下降占总收益的相当一部分。这说明方法收益并不只来自“加了教师提示”，还来自对小模型RL训练动态的细致修正。\n\n由于给出的全文片段未包含完整结果表和所有具体数值，这里不硬填不确定数字。可以确认的是，实验比较对象包括蒸馏、on-policy/self-distillation风格基线以及纯RL基线；评估维度包括训练内外泛化、难题学习效果、以及若干组件消融。精读时应直接核对主表、ablation表和hard/easy
