When the student is much smaller than the teacher, standard distillation tends to over-concentrate the student on the teacher’s sharp modes, which helps imitation but often hurts out-of-distribution generalization. Pure RL avoids logit imitation, but it has a different pathology: on hard questions where all student rollouts fail, the advantage is zero and the sample effectively disappears from learning. The paper asks how to transfer teacher knowledge without matching teacher logits and without injecting teacher responses directly into the policy gradient, which would violate the on-policy assumption and cause drift.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but real. The paper is about post-training, yet the underlying issue is broader: how a small model can absorb information from a stronger model without collapsing onto the stronger model’s distribution. That matters for small-model pretraining, continual pretraining, RL-pretrain interfaces, and alternatives to classic distillation. The contribution is not a new RL foundation in the theoretical sense. It is a carefully designed recipe for knowledge transfer under tight student capacity, with a clear design principle: keep the teacher in the prompt, not in the gradient path. For researchers working on compact models, edge deployment, or RL as a substitute for distillation, that design choice is the main takeaway.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"$29","reading_guide":"$2a","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 prompt 构造和失败样例；“teacher in prompt, not gradients”是干净的训练信号边界。","en":"Read the prompt construction and failure cases; “teacher in prompts, not gradients” is a clean boundary for teacher-assisted RL signals."},"metadata":{"institutions":["NVIDIA","University of Washington","Allen Institute for AI"],"authors":["Byung-Kwan Lee","Ximing Lu","Shizhe Diao","Minki Kang","Saurav Muralidharan","Karan Sapra","Andrew Tao","Pavlo Molchanov","Yejin Choi","Yu-Chiang Frank Wang","Ryo Hachiuma"],"notable_authors":["Yejin Choi","Pavlo Molchanov","Andrew Tao"],"tags":["rl-post-training","knowledge-distillation","small-models","teacher-guidance"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18216","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.18216","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.17678","title":"See First, Answer Later: Visual Evidence Pre-Alignment via Sufficiency-Driven RL","abstract":"Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) integrate strong text reasoning with visual inputs, yet their responses can be inconsistent with the underlying images, indicating ineffective utilization of visual evidence during inference. The prevailing training paradigm relies on large-scale caption-based pretraining for general alignment, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to enable instruction following and complex reasoning. However, such pretraining provides only weak v","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T08:45:24Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17678.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 MLLM 在回答视觉问题时“看了图但没真正用好图”的问题。更具体地说，现有训练流程通常先做图文预训练，再做指令微调和 RL，但预训练阶段主要依赖短 caption，监督信号偏粗，模型容易学到场景级对齐，却学不到对细粒度属性、关系、局部区域和题目相关证据的稳定提取能力。结果是在推理时，模型更像是在做 P(a|q) 的语言先验补全，而不是可靠地做 P(a|v,q)。作者想解决的不是单个 benchmark 上的答题技巧，而是把“先找足够的视觉证据，再作答”变成一个独立训练目标，并把它放在 pretrain 与 post-train 之间，作为专门补强视觉 grounding 的中间阶段。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有两点。第一，它提出了一个清楚的训练接口：把视觉感知能力的补强从任务级回答监督里拆出来，单独优化一个 question-conditioned evidence policy。这种做法对多模态预训练很有启发，因为它不是继续堆更密的 caption，也不是直接在答案上做更强监督，而是引入一个中间变量，把“看见什么”与“如何回答”分开。第二，它给出了一种较低标注成本的路径：利用现成 QA 数据，通过 frozen blind reader 判断证据是否足以恢复答案，从而把 answer supervision 转成 evidence supervision。这个思路对预训练—后训练接口、latent reasoning、以及多模态 RL 信号设计都有参考价值。量级上，它更像是一个训练范式上的中等强度贡献，而不是基础架构改写；价值在于训练目标设计和阶段划分比较干净。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 VEPA，Visual Evidence Pre-Alignment。作者把原来的直接回答分解成两步：先由 MLLM 根据图像和问题生成文本形式的 visual evidence，再由一个只看问题和证据、完全不看图的 blind reader 去恢复答案。形式上，相当于把 P(a|v,q) 分解为 P(e|v,q) 和 P(a|e,q)。这里 e 被当作信息瓶颈，训练目标要求它同时满足两个条件：一是 sufficiency，即给定问题后，证据里要有足够信息让 reader 推出正确答案；二是 visual dependence，即证据必须真的依赖图像，而不是把答案直接泄露出来，或者退化成模板化空话。\n\n训练上作者采用 GRPO 来优化 evidence generation policy。每个样本会生成一组候选 evidence，由 group-relative 的方式做相对奖励学习。奖励设计是全文最关键的部分，不只是“reader 答对就给分”。作者显式加入了几类约束来避免训练走偏：其一，防止 answer leakage，避免模型直接把答案或近似答案塞进 evidence，导致 reader 成为形式上的验证器；其二，惩罚重复、冗余、退化的 evidence，避免 RL 常见的 verbosity 或 pattern collapse；其三，强调 sufficiency 而不是最终 answer generation，把视觉 grounding 与回答策略解耦。这样做的原因很明确：如果直接对最终答案做 RL，模型仍可能靠语言先验、题目模式或 shortcut 获得奖励，无法保证视觉证据真的被编码。\n\n从训练流程看，VEPA 被放在 caption pretraining 之后、SFT/RL 之前，属于一个中间对齐阶段。它不要求额外的人类 dense caption 标注，而是复用现有 VQA/QA 数据。与“直接用更长 caption 继续预训练”相比，VEPA 的差异在于监督是 question-conditioned 的。也就是说，模型不是被要求穷举描述整张图，而是学习围绕当前问题抽取足够且必要的视觉证据。这比静态 dense caption 更接近推理时的真实信息需求，也更节省表示带宽。","experiments":"从引言和方法部分能确认的实验结论是：VEPA 在多类视觉要求较高的评测上都优于标准训练 recipe，覆盖 knowledge-intensive VQA、compositional VQA、fine-grained perception 和 holistic multimodal evaluation，并且收益不仅出现在单一 backbone。作者还强调，增益来自视觉感知增强，而不是额外任务监督，因为训练只引入了 evidence-level RL 目标，没有增加新的人工标注类型。\n\n全文片段里没有给出完整表格数字、参数量和数据规模，因此不能编造具体提升幅度。但从作者描述的 ablation 重点可以提炼出几条关键信息：第一，blind reader 作为冻结评估器是必要的，它让奖励聚焦在“证据是否足够”，而不是让同一个模型自洽打分；第二，answer leakage 抑制项是必要的，否则 evidence 会退化成隐式答案通道；第三，性能提升在 OOD 数据上仍能保留，说明学到的不是 benchmark-specific 的答题模板，而是更稳定的视觉 grounding。若阅读全文，最值得核对的是主表里不同 benchmark 的提升分布、reward 各项消融、以及 evidence 质量的定性案例，因为这些决定了方法到底是在提升感知，还是只是在改变输出风格。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读方法设计，而不是逐行抠公式。建议先看 Introduction 和 Preliminary，抓住作者为什么要把视觉证据单独建模，以及 sufficiency / visual dependence 这两个约束分别在防什么问题。然后重点看方法部分里 reward 设计和 GRPO 训练细节，尤其是如何定义 answer leakage、如何处理重复和退化 evidence、group sampling 怎么做。实验部分优先看三类内容：主结果表、reward/reader/阶段位置的消融、以及 evidence 可视化案例。阅读时间大约 25 到 40 分钟。如果你关心多模态预训练接口，值得额外想两个问题：一是 evidence 作为文本瓶颈会不会限制视觉信息上限；二是这种中间阶段能否推广到视频、语音或 unified multimodal pretraining。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a persistent failure mode in MLLMs: they answer visual questions with weak grounding, even when the image is available. The authors argue that the standard recipe—caption-based pretraining followed by instruction tuning and RL—builds only coarse alignment. Short captions emphasize salient objects and global scene summaries, but they do not train the model to reliably extract fine-grained, question-relevant evidence such as attributes, relations, and small regions. As a result, inference often drifts toward language-prior-driven answering rather than genuine image-conditioned reasoning. The paper tries to fix this by introducing an explicit intermediate objective: before learning how to answer better, train the model to produce sufficient visual evidence for the question.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the main contribution is not a new VQA trick but a cleaner training decomposition. The work inserts a dedicated stage between multimodal pretraining and post-training, and optimizes a question-conditioned evidence policy instead of relying on answer supervision to indirectly improve perception. That is a useful design pattern for multimodal foundation models: separate 'what visual information should be extracted' from 'how to produce the final answer.' A second contribution is the supervision mechanism. The method reuses existing QA data and converts answer labels into evidence-level rewards through a frozen blind reader, avoiding the need for dense human annotations. The impact is moderate but real: this is a training-objective and stage-design contribution, with implications for multimodal grounding, latent-variable formulations, and RL signal design at the pretrain/post-train boundary.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"$2c","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, so the goal is to understand the training idea rather than every implementation detail. Start with the Introduction and Preliminary sections. They explain why caption pretraining is insufficient and why the paper introduces a latent evidence variable. Then focus on the method section, especially the reward construction: how sufficiency is measured, how answer leakage is detected, and how repetitive evidence is discouraged. In experiments, prioritize the main benchmark table, the ablations on reward components and blind-reader design, and the qualitative evidence examples. Expected reading time is around 25–40 minutes. If you work on multimodal pretraining, two follow-up questions are worth keeping in mind: whether a textual evidence bottleneck limits the ceiling of visual information, and whether the same intermediate stage could be generalized to video, audio, or unified multimodal pretraining.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 reward 定义和 ablation；若控制干净，它支持“先证据对齐再推理”的 VLM 后训练 recipe。","en":"Read the reward definition and ablations; if the controls are clean, it supports a useful VLM recipe of evidence alignment before reasoning."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yilian Liu","Sicong Leng","Guoshun Nan","Junyi Zhu","Jiayu Huang","Minghao Sun","Xuancheng Zhu","Yisong Chen","Zexian Wei","Xiaofeng Tao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MLLM","visual-grounding","GRPO","post-training","evidence-alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17678","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.17678","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.17199","title":"PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation","abstract":"Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-15T18:37:51Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17199.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是 on-policy distillation 的训练不稳定问题。标准 OPD 用 student 自己采样出的 token 来估计 reverse-KL，避免全词表计算，但单样本 Monte Carlo 估计会把每个 token 的更新权重变成 teacher-student 的 log-prob ratio。这个 reward 没有上下界，低概率 token 上的偶发事件会产生极大梯度，导致训练早期性能下降、生成长度震荡，以及收敛后仍明显落后于 full-vocabulary OPD。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在于训练信号诊断，而不只是一个 post-training trick。它指出了一个常被忽略的问题：当语言模型训练目标从全分布 loss 变成采样轨迹上的 policy-gradient 形式后，目标的无偏性并不保证优化条件良好。对 RL pretrain、在线蒸馏、self-improvement 和 annealing 阶段都有参考意义。贡献量级主要体现在两点：一是把 vanilla OPD 的失败归因到 reward-level 的无界 log-ratio，而不是简单归因到学习率或 batch size；二是给出一个 bounded、sign-consistent 的替代 reward，在保持 sampled-token 低成本的同时接近甚至超过 full-vocabulary OPD。","technical_approach":"方法叫 PowerOPD。核心做法是保留 OPD 中“teacher 分数减 student 分数”的结构，但把原来的 log p 换成 Box-Cox power transform。形式上使用 h(π_T) - h(π_θ)，其中 h 是严格单调函数，因此只要 teacher 对某个 token 的概率高于 student，reward 符号就仍然为正；反之为负。这保证了训练方向不反。标准 OPD 的 log-ratio 可以看作 α → 0 的极限；当 α > 0 时，reward 被限制在有限范围内，从源头上削掉低概率 token 造成的极端权重。这个设计和 reward clipping、tanh、z-score normalization 的区别在于：后处理方法是在 log-ratio 已经把信号扭曲之后再压缩，而 PowerOPD 直接改变 probability-to-reward mapping。代价是它不再是原始 reverse-KL 的简单单样本估计，而是一个更适合在线优化的有界 surrogate objective。论文也观察到 α 越大，越会压制 teacher 和 student 都认为低概率的 token，把学习信号集中到至少一方认为可能的 token 上。","experiments":"实验集中在数学推理蒸馏。模型来自 Qwen3 family，包括 0.6B 和 1.7B student，以及 4B 和 8B teacher，共四组 teacher-student 配置，评测覆盖六个数学 benchmark。在 Qwen3-1.7B-Base student、Qwen3-4B teacher、MATH-500 的代表性设置中，vanilla OPD 收敛后只有 54.93% accuracy，比 full-vocabulary OPD 低 8.19 points；PowerOPD 相比 vanilla OPD 提升 9.6 points，并且用约 1/10 的训练步数达到相同 accuracy。完整评测中，PowerOPD 的 benchmark-averaged Avg@8 / Pass@8 相比 vanilla OPD 最高提升 +6.37 / +5.71；相比 clipping、tanh、z-score 等后处理稳定化方法最高提升 +3.01 / +3.54；相比 full-vocabulary OPD 最高提升 +2.59 / +8.90。单项 benchmark 的提升最高分别达到 +16.75 / +15.00、+8.43 / +7.50、+11.60 / +25.00。效率上，相比 full-vocabulary OPD，PowerOPD 每步 wall-clock time 降低 59.2%，peak GPU memory 降低 23.1%。训练动态分析显示，PowerOPD 的 gradient norm 比 vanilla OPD 初始 spike 低 3000 倍以上，response length 震荡也更小。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30 到 45 分钟。重点看 reward-level diagnosis、Power transformation 的定义、α 的消融，以及 gradient/length dynamics 图。最值得注意的是它如何区分“无偏估计”和“可优化训练信号”：vanilla OPD 的 Monte Carlo 估计在目标上干净，但在 token-level reward 上病态。对做 RL pretrain 或在线蒸馏的人，α scaling 的实验比最终 accuracy 更有信息量，因为它说明低概率 token 的处理方式会直接改变生成分布的稳定性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why sampled-token on-policy distillation becomes unstable in LLM post-training. Vanilla OPD estimates a reverse-KL objective from student-sampled tokens, avoiding a full-vocabulary computation. The resulting policy-gradient weight is the teacher-student log-probability ratio. This reward is unbounded, so rare low-probability events can dominate the update, especially at early sequence positions. The observed symptoms are degraded validation accuracy at the beginning of training, large response-length oscillations, and a sizable gap to exact full-vocabulary OPD after convergence.","importance":"The useful part for pretraining and large-scale optimization is the training-signal diagnosis. The paper makes a concrete point: an unbiased sampled estimator of a distribution-level objective can still be a poor optimization signal once it is used on model-generated trajectories. This matters for online distillation, RL-style pretraining, self-improvement loops, and cooldown or annealing stages where the model trains on its own generations. The contribution is not just a stabilization heuristic. It identifies the unbounded log-ratio reward as the failure mode and replaces it with a bounded, direction-preserving reward that keeps the computational profile of sampled OPD.","technical_approach":"PowerOPD keeps the transform-then-subtract structure of OPD: h(π_T) - h(π_θ). If h is strictly increasing, the sign of the reward still matches the teacher-student probability gap. A token receives positive reward when the teacher assigns higher probability than the student, and negative reward in the opposite case. The paper instantiates h with a Box-Cox power transform. The usual log-ratio reward appears as the α → 0 limit; for any α > 0, the reward becomes bounded. This differs from clipping, tanh compression, or z-score normalization, which operate after the log-ratio has already produced extreme values. PowerOPD changes the probability-to-reward map itself. The tradeoff is that the method is better viewed as a bounded surrogate objective rather than a plain single-sample estimator of the original reverse-KL. Larger α suppresses tokens that both models assign low probability and concentrates learning on tokens considered plausible by either the teacher or the student.","experiments":"The experiments use math-reasoning distillation with Qwen3-family teacher-student pairs: 0.6B and 1.7B students, and 4B and 8B teachers, evaluated on six math benchmarks. In the representative Qwen3-1.7B-Base student and Qwen3-4B teacher setting on MATH-500, vanilla OPD converges to 54.93% accuracy, 8.19 points below full-vocabulary OPD. PowerOPD improves over vanilla OPD by 9.6 points and reaches the same accuracy level with about 10× fewer training steps. Across the benchmark suite, the best averaged Avg@8 / Pass@8 gains are +6.37 / +5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01 / +3.54 over post-hoc reward stabilization, and +2.59 / +8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD. The largest single-benchmark gains reach +16.75 / +15.00, +8.43 / +7.50, and +11.60 / +25.00 under the corresponding comparisons. Compared with full-vocabulary OPD, PowerOPD reduces wall-clock time per step by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Gradient tracking shows that its norms stay more than 3000× below the initial spike of vanilla OPD.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30 to 45 minutes. Read the reward diagnosis first, then the Box-Cox transformation, the α ablations, and the gradient and response-length dynamics. The most transferable lesson is the separation between estimator correctness and optimization quality. For readers working on RL pretraining or online distillation, the α-scaling results are more informative than the headline benchmark numbers, because they show how probability-to-reward shaping changes the stability of generated trajectories.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看的是“失稳根因在 reward 形状，不在采样器细节”；先读 reward 分析和 ablation，若 setup 干净，内部 RL/蒸馏都该试有界目标。","en":"Worth reading for the claim that OPD instability comes from reward geometry, not just sampling noise; read the reward analysis and ablations first, then test bounded objectives in your own RL/distillation stack."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Anhao Zhao","Junlong Tong","Yingqi Fan","Ping Nie","Wenjie Li","Xiaoyu Shen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["on-policy-distillation","reverse-KL","reward-shaping","training-stability","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.17199","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.17581","title":"Visored: A Controlled-Natural-Language Prover for LLM-Generated Mathematics","abstract":"We present a dependent-type-based prover designed around the way LLMs (and humans) tend to write mathematics, complementing existing systems such as Lean and Rocq. loop、dense reward signal 和 data generator。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the verification gap for LLM-generated mathematics. Systems such as Lean and Rocq provide strong kernel-checked guarantees, but they expect already formalized statements and proofs. LLMs and humans usually produce informal or semi-formal mathematical prose, where types, coercions, side conditions, library choices, and edge-case conventions are often implicit. Direct autoformalization into Lean is therefore brittle and can silently change the meaning of the original proof.","importance":"The paper is not primarily a base-pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to mathematical reasoning post-training, verified data generation, verifier design, and reward construction. Visored introduces a controlled natural language layer backed by a dependently typed intermediate representation. This lets the model write in a format closer to natural mathematical text while forcing hidden semantic commitments to become explicit. The design can provide more localized feedback than a single Lean compilation failure, which matters for training proof generators and building checked reasoning corpora.","technical_approach":"Visored is a controlled-natural-language prover for mathematical proofs written in LaTeX-like prose. Its accepted sentence templates and LaTeX commands are specified through external configuration files rather than being hard-coded into the parser. The input is elaborated into a dependently typed IR where subtype choices, casts, named lemmas, edge-case conventions, and domain restrictions are represented explicitly. The elaborator enforces well-definedness in the style of PVS: expressions such as 1/x, sqrt(x), and log(x) require proof obligations for x≠0, x≥0, and x>0 respectively. Each “Then ...” step is discharged by a cost-bounded rule-driven solver intended to close routine steps that a textbook proof would omit. Accepted proofs can be emitted as Lean 4 for kernel-level rechecking, although Visored’s own acceptance decision is not routed through Lean.","experiments":"The experiments are positioned as an early prototype study around miniF2F, covering middle-school-level set theory, algebra, and inequalities. The empirical focus is not large-scale model training or a head-to-head replacement of Lean tactic search. It is a feasibility check of the workflow: the LLM supplies a controlled-natural-language proof outline, while Visored performs type checking, well-definedness checking, routine proof closure, and localized diagnostics.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 30–40 minute skim. trace","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T11:27:28Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17803.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在严格在线（streaming）场景里，模型每道题只见一次、没有外部标注、也不允许全量离线重训时，如何把推理时产生的大量“思考轨迹/探索”转化为可复用的长期能力增益，同时避免遗忘与训练不稳定。","importance":"这篇更像“post-train 参数量级）。\n关键设计动机有三层：\n1）反对“文本瓶颈记忆”：只存 CoT 文本会丢掉隐藏态里更抽象的结构信息，也容易把偶然的表面模式当成可迁移知识；作者用实验声称 raw-trace ICL 与自反思式文本提炼都难带来稳健泛化。\n2）用内部奖励做自监督：借鉴无监督/自奖励 RL full finetuning with ~0.001% additional parameters, shows cross-dataset transfer, and scales with accumulated experience.\nThese should be validated against the experimental section: strength of baselines (self-consistency/verifiers/tools), the exact definition of internal reward, and whether the online protocol truly prevents leakage and repeated exposure.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (25–40 的高方差方向，但这些方向同时和 retain set 共享，也正是攻击者微调时最容易恢复的方向。因此，遗忘、能力保持、抗恢复三者的冲突有可观测的表示机制。这个视角对 open-weight 模型的合规删除、continual pretrain 后的知识移除、以及训练后安全约束是否真正改变模型内部能力，都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法名为 biology 数据。第二类是 BeaverTails 的 animal_abuse 行为倾向删除，retain set 通过把有害概念替换成 benign counterpart 构造，以尽量保留上下文分布。模型包括 Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 3.5 9B、Gemma 4 E4B 和 DeepSeek-V2-Lite，覆盖 dense 与 MoE。所有方法共享 disruption unlearning: removing targeted knowledge or behavioral tendencies while preserving general capability and preventing recovery through fine-tuning or few-shot prompting.","importance":"Its main value is the representation-level diagnosis of why many unlearning methods remain reversible. The paper argues that standard objectives mostly update high-variance forget-set directions. Those directions are also shared with retain data and overlap with the subspace a fine-tuning attacker can recover. This gives a concrete mechanism behind the tension among forgetting, low disruption, and robustness. The framing is useful for open-weight compliance, post-training safety interventions, and continual-pretraining settings where knowledge must be removed rather than hidden at the surface behavior level.","technical_approach":"RepSelect applies singular value decomposition to forget-set weight gradients before each unlearning update and suppresses the top principal components. The update is then restricted toward lower-variance directions that carry weaker individual forgetting signals but are less entangled with general capability and less aligned with the attacker’s relearning subspace. The analysis uses the MLP update decomposition ΔW=Σ g_t⊗a_t, separating token-level input activations from output gradients. Compared with gradient ascent, KL-regularized unlearning, NPO, or RMU-style methods, the key change is not a new retain loss but a selection of which representation directions are allowed to drive the unlearning update.","experiments":"$2e","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is enough. We formulate it as a stochastic estimation problem under information constraints, decomposing prediction risk into two components: an intrinsic limit (static data-model compatibility) and a reducible optimization variance. optimizati","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T08:07:42Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17649.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是：在完整微调开始前，最终性能到底能被预测到什么程度。它不把问题当成普通回归，而是把微调结果看成一个随机变量，随机性来自数据顺序、优化噪声、非确定性算子等因素。核心问题是区分两类误差：一类来自模型和数据的静态匹配程度，短探测也消不掉；另一类来自优化过程尚未展开导致的不确定性，可以通过少量 pretrain、mid-training、数据混合筛选等场景：有些不确定性来自数据-模型兼容性，有些来自早期优化轨迹，二者不应混在一个黑盒预测分数里。","technical_approach":"论文把一个微调任务定义为 T=(M,D,A)，其中 M 是预训练模型，D 是下游数据，A 是随机优化算法。完整执行任务后得到性能 R，R 被视为条件分布 P(R|T) 下的随机变量。给定预算 c，预测器可用的信息集为 I_c={X_s, X_d(c)}：X_s 是零或低成本的静态信息，例如数据和模型的兼容性信号；X_d(c) 是运行早期优化后得到的动态信息，例如 loss trajectory、梯度统计等。最优预测器用 Bayes predictor E[R|I_c,T] 表示。基于这个设定，论文将预测风险分解为计算无关的 intrinsic limit 和随 probing 预算下降的 optimization variance。进一步，作者用随机逼近过程的约束说明 optimization variance 的下降速度存在下界，不能假设短探测能任意快地消除不确定性。由此得到一个预算最优 probing 原则：继续探测是否划算，取决于边际风险下降和边际计算成本之间的关系。最后，论文用 predictability phase diagram correlation 或 compute saving 的可核验数字。","reading_guide":"泛读建议约 20 到 30 分钟。优先看问题设定和风险分解部分，确认 I_c、Bayes predictor、task-conditional risk 的定义。其次看 uncertainty decay 的下界推导，它决定了这篇论文和普通经验性预测工作的区别。最后看 budget-optimal probing 和 phase diagram。若只关心工程落地，可以跳过较细的证明，重点理解两个问题：哪些特征属于 static compatibility，哪些信号属于 optimization dynamics；以及继续做 probe 的停止条件如何由边际收益决定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how predictable a fine-tuning run is before paying for the full run. It treats the final score as a random variable induced by the pretrained model, the dataset, and the stochastic optimizer. The main distinction is between uncertainty that is intrinsic to the data-model pair and uncertainty that comes from not yet observing enough of the optimization trajectory.","importance":"The useful part for pretraining and post-training research is the risk decomposition, not a new fine-tuning recipe. It gives a cleaner way to reason about go/no-go decisions, short probing runs, and compute allocation. The same framing can inform continual pretraining or mid-training decisions, where static data-model fit and early optimization signals are often mixed into one empirical score.","technical_approach":"A fine-tuning task is represented as T=(M,D,A), with M the pretrained model, D the dataset, and A the stochastic training algorithm. Running the task produces a scalar outcome R sampled from P(R|T). At probing budget c, the predictor observes an information set I_c={X_s, X_d(c)}, where X_s contains static signals such as dataset or compatibility features, and X_d(c) contains dynamic signals revealed by partial optimization. The Bayes-optimal predictor is E[R|I_c,T). The paper decomposes the optimal prediction risk into a computation-independent intrinsic limit and a reducible optimization-induced variance. It then argues that the reducible part cannot decay arbitrarily fast; its decay is constrained by stochastic approximation dynamics. This leads to a compute-aware probing rule based on marginal uncertainty reduction versus marginal cost, and to a predictability phase diagram that separates intrinsically ambiguous tasks from tasks that only need more dynamic information.","experiments":"The contribution is primarily theoretical. The central results are the risk decomposition, the lower-bound style constraint on uncertainty decay, and the budget-optimal probing condition. The material does not provide concrete empirical numbers such as model sizes, dataset sizes, RMSE, rank correlation, or measured compute savings. The framework is best read as an explanation and organization tool for probing-based predictors rather than as a new empirical predictor.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 20–30 minute skim. addresses a practical fine-tuning decision problem: given a dataset and hyperparameter setting, predict the final evaluation score before running the full fine-tuning job, and provide enough diagnostic signal to decide whether the run should proceed.","importance":"The paper is relevant because it separates two useful sources of signal: static dataset descriptors and early optimization behavior. This separation is also useful beyond supervised fine-tuning, including continued pretraining data selection and mid-training task allocation. Static features describe the data before training; probe features reveal whether the current model can actually learn from that data under the chosen recipe.","technical_approach":"Each candidate run is encoded as a meta-feature vector for a dataset-hyperparameter pair. The target is the final score obtained after full fine-tuning and evaluation. The feature set combines static dataset descriptors, such as size and lexical diversity, with dynamic probe features from a short standardized run, such as early loss decay and gradient stability. A lightweight gradient-boosting predictor maps these features to a continuous performance estimate. A threshold can then be applied as a deployment policy for go/no-go decisions. SHAP attribution is used to explain which features drive the prediction, making the system more diagnostic than a single proxy score. Compared with early-stop extrapolation, the method does not rely only on short-horizon curves. Compared with proxy-model approaches, it explicitly includes dataset-level descriptors and interpretability.","experiments":"The empirical study includes more than 1,300 fine-tuning runs using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, evaluated on MMLU and several task types. TuneAhead is reported to outperform Early-Stop Extrapolation, ProxyLM, and an Early-Dynamics baseline. The paper also gives an illustrative compute-saving case: training all 10 candidates costs about 30 GPU-hours, while filtering predicted failures first reduces the cost to about 18.7 GPU-hours, a 37.4% saving for a pool with 4 successful and 6 failed candidates. The realized saving depends on the success threshold and the composition of the candidate pool. The available material does not include the full numeric error tables, so the absolute prediction accuracy should be checked in the experimental section before using the method operationally.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25–35 minutes. Start with the task formulation to confirm that the target is the final benchmark score for a dataset-hyperparameter pair, not the training loss. training-free 的，说明模型内部已经包含可恢复的视觉证据，推理时只要识别有害覆盖并回收中间层信号，就能改善冲突任务表现。这对理解“模型知道但没说出来”的现象很有启发，也和 LLM 中间层 logit lens、early exit、representation steering 一类思路形成呼应。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是诊断。作者把每一层隐藏状态投影到输出词表，观察答案分布如何随深度变化。为量化视觉与文本哪一方占优，他们定义了 Modal Dominance Ratio，核心是比较图像支持答案 w_vis 和文本支持答案 w_text 在当前层输出分布中的相对偏好。这样可以得到一条 layer-wise modal preference 曲线。结果显示，在不少 conflict-incorrect 样本中，MDR 在中间层仍偏向视觉，到了后层才翻转为偏向文本。作者进一步发现，单纯发生“后层转向”并不等于错误，因为成功样本也会发生转向；真正有判别力的是转向方向：失败样本大多朝文本方向移动，成功样本大多朝视觉方向移动。第二部分是修正。作者提出 CALRD，核心思想是找到输出分布变化最剧烈的 transition layer，把它当作尚未被覆盖的锚点层。然后用两个信号决定是否以及多大程度把该层 logits 混回最终输出：anchor confidence 衡量锚点层预测有多确定，prediction retention 衡量该预测是否保留到最终层。如果锚点层很自信，但最终层没有保住这个预测，就判定为可能发生了有害覆盖，于是对最终 logits 做自适应融合；如果后层变化看起来是有益 refinement，则尽量不干预。与直接 ensemble 多层输出不同，CALRD 的关键在于它只在检测到“高置信锚点 + 低保留”的覆盖模式时介入，因此目标是纠正后层错误重写，而不是普遍削弱深层推理。","experiments":"论文构建了 Conflict-VQA 作为诊断基准，共 5,969 个样本，带有显式的 (w_vis, w_text) 标注，并为同一图像问题配对一致上下文和冲突上下文。数据来自 VrR-VG 图像和 TDIUC 问题，覆盖 object presence、color、attribute、counting、spatial reasoning、activity recognition 六类。机制分析里最关键的数字有两个：在失败样本中，85% 的预测转向朝文本方向；在成功样本中，89% 的转向朝视觉方向。这说明“转向方向”比“是否转向”更有诊断价值。方法评测覆盖五个 MLLM，从 InstructBLIP 到 Qwen3-VL，说明现象并非某一架构特有。CALRD 在冲突任务上带来最高 9.4% 的提升，同时对常规非冲突性能影响较小。阅读正文时建议重点核对三类实验：一，MDR 曲线在不同模型上的一致性；二，anchor confidence 和 prediction retention 两个信号各自的消融，以及不用自适应门控时是否会伤害正常样本；三，Conflict-VQA 之外是否在已有冲突 benchmark 上复现同样趋势。如果论文给了按题型拆分结果，颜色、属性、计数这类更依赖局部视觉证据的类别通常最能说明问题。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精读。先看方法分析部分，弄清 MDR 的定义和 transition layer 的定位方式，这是整篇的支点。然后看 Figure 1 一类的层间轨迹图，直观看模型如何先偏向视觉再被文本拉回。接着读 CALRD 的公式和伪代码，重点不是融合本身，而是它如何区分“有害覆盖”和“有益后层修正”。实验部分优先看 Conflict-VQA 构造、主结果表、以及两个诊断信号的消融。预计 30-45 paper asks a mechanistic question behind a well-known MLLM failure mode: when image evidence conflicts with text, why do models often follow the text? Prior work mostly documented the behavior. The key unresolved issue is where the failure happens. Is visual information never encoded well, lost during fusion, or correctly formed and then overwritten later? The paper’s main finding is that many failures belong to the third category. In a substantial fraction of conflict cases, intermediate layers already favor the correct vision-grounded answer, but later layers reverse that preference and produce the text-supported answer. The authors call this late-layer textual override.","importance":"Its contribution is strongest as a mechanistic analysis with a practical decoding consequence. For multimodal foundation models, it moves textual bias from a surface-level behavioral observation to an internal processing diagnosis: the problem is often not that the model cannot see, but that later computation does not preserve what it has seen. That distinction matters because it changes the intervention space. If the issue were weak visual encoding, one would focus on representation learning or cross-modal alignment. If the issue is late overwrite, then layerwise retention, decoding, objective design, and post-training data balance become more relevant. The second important point is that the proposed fix is training-free. This suggests that useful visual evidence is already present inside the model and can be recovered at inference time, which connects naturally to broader ideas such as logit lens analysis, early-exit behavior, and representation steering.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"$31","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示了 MLLM 内部视觉特征在深层被文本特征覆盖的机制，对多模态对齐和 decoding 策略有启发，值得看其 layer-wise 分析。","en":"Reveals the mechanism of 'late-layer textual override' in MLLMs, offering insights for multimodal alignment and decoding strategies; the layer-wise analysis is worth reading."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xingming Li","Ao Cheng","Qiyao Sun","Xixiang He","Xuanyu Ji","Runke Huang","Qingyong Hu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MLLM","Textual Bias","Mechanistic Interpretability","Decoding Strategy"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17953","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.17953","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.17648","title":"From Brewing to Resolution: Tracing the Internal Lifecycle of Code Reasoning in LLMs","abstract":"Standard accuracy metrics cannot explain why LLMs handle variable tracking but fail on semantically equivalent loops. $(","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T01:16:26Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17399.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解释小型 Transformer 在 modular multiplication grokking 中到底学到了什么。已有分析用普通 additive Fourier basis 观察 embedding，发现频谱很密，好像模型需要所有频率才能表示乘法。论文指出，这主要是分析基不匹配造成的：乘法任务的自然结构不是整数标签上的加法循环，而是有限域非零元素构成的乘法群。","importance":"对大模型预训练的直接工程价值有限，但对机制分析有清晰启发。它说明在研究模型内部表示时，选择错误的坐标系会把简单结构看成复杂结构。这个结论可以迁移到更大的语言模型分析中：如果任务或数据有隐含群结构、组合结构或 token 重标号对称性，直接在原始 token index 上做频谱或线性探针，可能会低估表示的稀疏性和可解释性。论文的贡献是把 modular multiplication 的 grokking 重新解释为“通过离散对数把乘法变成加法”，而不是学习一个难以解释的 dense Fourier 算法。","technical_approach":"实验任务是 a·b mod 113，排除 0，因此输入集合 {1,...,112} 在模 113 乘法下形成循环群。选择 primitive root g=3 后，每个元素 a 都能写成 3^α mod 113，离散对数 log_g(a)=α 给出从乘法群到 Z/112Z 加法群的同构。在这个重标号下，a·b 的乘法关系变成 α+β mod 112。因此，论文不再直接对原始整数标签做 additive DFT，而是先按离散对数顺序重排 embedding，再做标准 DFT。这等价于在乘法群上做 multiplicative character transform。分析包括三部分：embedding frequency spectrum、MLP neuron 的二维频率归属，以及 embedding SVD 后按离散对数顺序观察主成分。","experiments":"模型是 1-layer decoder-only AdamW，learning rate 1e-3，weight decay 1.0，全 batch 训练 40,000 epochs。训练动态符合 grokking：约 500 epochs 记住训练集，测试损失仍高；约 9,000 到 14,000 epochs 测试损失突然下降到接近 0。在表示分析中，普通 additive basis 下 embedding 频谱接近均匀，Gini coefficient 为 0.071；multiplicative character basis 下能量集中到 4 个关键频率 {2, 8, 47, 56}，Gini coefficient 为 0.579，约为前者 8.1 倍。MLP neurons 在乘法字符基下也更容易按频率聚类，SVD 主成分按离散对数重排后呈现清晰的正弦结构。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 小模型，不应直接外推到自然语言；但“正确对齐任务对称性后，dense 表示可能变 sparse”这个分析原则值得保留。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what a small Transformer actually learns when it groks modular multiplication. Prior work found that the learned embedding appears dense under the standard additive Fourier basis, suggesting that multiplication requires many Fourier components. The paper argues that this is a basis mismatch. Modular multiplication should be analyzed on the multiplicative group of nonzero residues, not on the raw integer labels with the usual additive DFT.","importance":"The result is mainly useful for mechanistic interpretability rather than for large-scale pretraining recipes. Its practical lesson is that an internal representation can look dense and hard to interpret if the analysis uses the wrong coordinate system. For LLM analysis, the same warning applies to token-index spectra, probes, or representation decompositions when the underlying task has a hidden symmetry or relabeling structure. The paper reframes modular multiplication grokking as a simple algorithm: use a discrete-log coordinate system, reduce multiplication to addition, and then use a sparse Fourier-like representation.","technical_approach":"The task is a·b mod 113 over nonzero residues. Since 113 is prime, {1,...,112} forms a cyclic multiplicative group. With primitive root g=3, each element a can be written as 3^α mod 113, and the discrete logarithm log_g(a)=α maps the multiplicative group to the additive group Z/112Z. Under this relabeling, multiplication becomes addition of exponents. The analysis therefore reorders embedding rows by discrete log and then applies a standard DFT. This is the multiplicative character transform on the group. The paper applies this basis to the token embeddings, to two-dimensional MLP neuron activation patterns over all input pairs, and to SVD components of the learned embedding matrix.","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"Skim in 20 to 30 minutes. C(θ) 在所有训练轨迹上保持不变，就要求 C 的梯度与训练损失诱导的向量场正交。作者沿用并扩展 Marcotte 系列工作的框架，在一定损失和数据假设下给出完整性结果，即证明列出的守恒量不只是一些例子，而是穷尽了所有满足条件的光滑守恒律。具体覆盖三类模块：第一，带 GELU、SiLU、SwiGLU 的前馈网络，处理平滑激活和门控激活相对于 ReLU gradient-flow training trajectories in modern neural architectures, and whether these invariants can be characterized completely. The target is not the classical linear or ReLU setting, but modules used in current large models: GELU, SiLU and SwiGLU feed-forward blocks, multi-head attention, sinusoidal positional encodings, RoPE, and MoE layers with different gating mechanisms.","importance":"The value for pretraining research is in training dynamics and implicit bias, not in an immediate recipe change. Conservation laws describe geometric constraints imposed jointly by the architecture and the optimizer. They can help reason about parameter-scale constraints, stability, and routing dynamics. The contribution is mainly theoretical: it extends the Marcotte-style characterization framework from simpler networks and single-head attention to components that are much closer to LLM practice, especially multi-head attention and MoE.","technical_approach":"The paper formulates conservation-law discovery as a system of partial differential equations. For a smooth function C(θ) to be conserved under gradient flow, its gradient must be orthogonal to the vector field induced by the training loss. Under assumptions inherited from prior characterization work, the authors prove completeness: the listed invariants exhaust the admissible smooth conservation laws, rather than being isolated examples. The analysis covers three families. First, feed-forward networks with smooth and gated activations, including GELU, SiLU, and SwiGLU. Second, multi-head attention, with extensions to sinusoidal positional encodings and rotary embeddings. Third, MoE architectures under dense and sparse regimes, including softmax, sigmoid, and normalized-sigmoid gating. The main distinction from earlier work is the move from illustrative invariants to complete characterizations for architectures used in modern large-scale models.","experiments":"The experiments validate the predicted invariants rather than optimize task performance. The paper tracks the relevant conserved quantities for the considered feed-forward, attention, and MoE architectures under both full-batch training and SGD. The described experimental suite includes small and larger multimodal datasets. The verifiable takeaway is that the invariants behave as predicted under full-batch gradient descent and remain approximately meaningful under SGD. The provided content does not contain reliable numeric details on model size, data scale, or measured drift, so the result should be read as theoretical validation with empirical checks rather than as an engineering benchmark.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 to 45 minutes. Start with Section 3 to understand the reduction from conservation laws to PDE constraints. Then read the three parts of Section 4: feed-forward blocks with modern activations, multi-head attention with positional encodings, and MoE gating. For LLM pretraining, Sections 4.2 and 4.3 are the most relevant. Section 5 is worth reading for proof ideas and limitations, especially before extrapolating gradient-flow results to AdamW, large batches, mixed precision, and distributed training. Checkpoint merging, model soups, continued-pretraining branch merging, and alignment of attention heads all depend on whether two solutions can be put into a comparable coordinate system. The paper makes a useful point: positional encodings change Transformer symmetries. Results derived for vanilla attention may overstate the available equivalence transformations, especially when RoPE is used.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes the non-injectivity of the parameter-to-function map θ ↦ f(·; θ). In classical networks, hidden-unit permutations preserve the function. In attention, the symmetry structure includes head permutations and transformations tied to the query, key, value, and output projections. The main addition is the treatment of positional encodings. Sinusoidal encodings, treated as part of the input representation, largely preserve the equivalence structure. RoPE is more restrictive because it applies position-dependent rotations to query and key vectors; admissible parameter transformations must be compatible with these rotations. This reshapes the equivalence classes compared with vanilla attention. The paper then applies these equivalences to linear mode connectivity: instead of interpolating two raw parameter vectors directly, it first aligns them through function-preserving transformations and then measures the loss barrier along the straight-line path.","experiments":"The experimental focus is the comparison between naive interpolation and interpolation after functional-equivalence alignment. The introductory figure illustrates the intended effect: a high-barrier naive path can become much smoother after matching. The paper also positions its method relative to existing alignment tools such as simulated annealing, optimal transport, Wasserstein barycenters, and activation matching. The provided content does not give reliable numeric details on model size, datasets, or barrier reduction, so the safe takeaway is qualitative: incorporating attention-specific equivalences, especially those constrained by positional encodings, improves the analysis of LMC.","reading_guide":"A productive skim takes about 25 to 40 minutes. Then focus on the sinusoidal positional encoding and RoPE results, since these determine whether the theory applies to current LLM architectures. Finally read the LMC application section, especially the loss-barrier definition, the alignment procedure, and the comparison between naive and matched interpolation paths. Continual pretraining, instruction tuning, and PEFT-based maintenance all face forgetting, but many mitigation methods regularize parameters or replay data without identifying the function-space directions that drift. This work shows that, in a useful linearized regime, forgetting is often concentrated in a small number of NTK eigenmodes. That suggests a more selective design space for replay, distillation, and adapter constraints: protect the vulnerable function subspace rather than penalizing all parameter movement uniformly.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"The empirical results support the function-space predictor in the regimes where the assumptions are appropriate. 的“信念/概率”在重复作答同一问题时是否满足贝叶斯预测序列应有的相干性条件（martingale / c.i.d.），以及如果不满足，能否通过推理时重采样或训练目标把信念过程稳定下来，从而得到更可校准的不确定性估计。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接改动不多，但对“训练目标如何塑造概率相干性与校准”有启发：它把 Falck 等人在玩具 ICL 任务里发现的 martingale 违背，搬到更贴近真实使用的多选 QA，并提出一种可操作的诊断协议（PPR）与一个可用于微调的相干性损失。若该损失在更广泛任务上成立，它可能成为一种面向校准/不确定性的后训练正则项，也可能反向提示预训练阶段的目标缺口（AR 训练并不保证序列预测的时间一致性）。","technical_approach":"核心设置是把输出空间限制为离散的多选集合 A，从而可以“精确”计算模型对每个选项的概率（直接读 logits），避免只靠采样估计分布。\n提出 Prompted Predictive Resampling（PPR）：让模型在同一上下文里连续生成一串对同一问题的答案（不是独立采样），并在每一步都计算对 A 的预测分布，观察这个分布序列是否满足 martingale（直观上：在看到自己先前的回答后，后验预测的期望应保持一致）。\n经验现象：早期步骤存在 belief drift（违反 的损失做微调，把“需要多步重采样才能稳定”的过程摊销到一次前向里（amortization），目标是改善校准而不损伤准确率。\n整体更像：诊断工具 + 推理时程序（多步自条件化）+ 可选的后训练正则。","experiments":"全文片段未给出具体基准名称、模型规模、以及校准指标（ECE/Brier/NLL 等）的数值结果，因此无法安全复述提升幅度。引言层面可确认的结论是：\n- 在标准多选 QA 上，PPR 观察到短期漂移、长期稳定；\n- 稳定后（通过足够 resampling 或通过 amortized 微调），不确定性校准变好且准确率不下降。\n阅读时需要重点核对：稳定需要多少步（成本是否可接受）、对不同温度/采样策略是否鲁棒、以及“稳定”是否只是趋向更尖锐/更保守的分布而非真正更校准。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 We present PreAct, which lets such an agent get faster on tasks it has done before. The first time it succeeds, PreAct compiles the run into a small state-machine program-states that check the screen, transitions that act-and on later runs replays it directly instead","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-16T13:40:21Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2606_17929.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有基于屏幕交互的计算机操作Agent每次执行重复任务时都从零启动完整的视觉-推理-行动循环，没有对历史成功经验的可验证复用能力，重复任务执行冗余成本高、效率低。","importance":"为Agent在使用过程中的效率自提升提供了轻量可落地的范式，核心的\"先校验再执行、入库前先验证\"设计思路对大模型调用缓存、技能复用类工作有直接参考价值，同时点出了当前Agent评测普遍忽略重复任务效率维度的问题。","technical_approach":"1. Its core design principles of 'check before acting, verify before storing' provide direct reference for LLM call caching and skill reuse systems, and highlights the missing repeated-task efficiency dimension in most current agent evaluation protocols.","technical_approach":"1. After an agent successfully completes a task for the first time, PreAct compiles the full trajectory into a lightweight finite state machine, where each state stores a screen state validation rule, and each transition stores a concrete executable action. 2. For subsequent matches to the same task, PreAct runs the state machine directly: it validates screen alignment with expected state before every action, invokes no LLM calls during successful replay, and immediately returns control to the base agent when a screen mismatch is detected. 3. Newly compiled state machines are only added to the skill library after a full independent replay that passes the benchmark's native task verifier, preventing accumulation of buggy programs that cause performance degradation over time.","experiments":"Tested across three platforms: AndroidWorld (mobile), OSWorld (desktop), WebArena (web). Warm replays of matched tasks run 8.5-13x faster than full agent inference with zero per-step LLM calls. The screen check mechanism improves success rate by 1.75-2.6 tasks across the cold-to-warm transition compared to blind replay without validation. Replacing LLM-based skill retrieval with a simple embedding retriever drops retrieval accuracy from 100% to 75.6% but has negligible impact on end performance at significantly lower cost. Factors including prompt wording and hand-written runtime guardrails show no measurable effect on results.","reading_guide":"Skim the paper. Focus on the comparison of limitations of prior skill reuse approaches in the introduction, the two-layer verification design, and the ablation results separating high-impact design choices from irrelevant ones. There is no need to dig into low-level implementation details of state machine construction. The advanced capabilities of models are realized through agent harnesses. Therefore, a gap between model assumptions and harness behavior can easily prevent the model's full capabilities from translating into agent performance.
